No mathematical dependence claimed between goal/corner quantities.

Direct OPEN returned cache miss. Domain separation does **not** prove collector independence.

Its fresh search index showed 27-match team-corner rates (Henan 5.63 and Tianjin 4.37), but its open cache was stale. - Period-specific / exact-game pregame corners **per fixture**, independent 27-match corners, and operator-defined credited-corner action are NOT_FULLY_VERIFIED. Earlier older aggregate snapshots cannot be silently treated as current.

**RESEARCH_ONLY / UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO / NOT_PRIMARY:** An explicit all-venue arithmetic comparison, using observed L20 form and the 27-match *search-result* corners aggregates, illustrates why completing research changes the uncertainty assessment:
- Henan L20 scoring rate 29/20=1.45; Tianjin L20 conceding rate 22/20=1.10. Naive home-goal proxy `(1.45+1.10)/2 = 1.275`.
- Tianjin L20 scoring rate 30/20=1.50; Henan L20 conceding rate 30/20=1.50. Naive away-goal proxy `(1.50+1.50)/2 = 1.500`; **total λ=2.775 goals**.
- At an assumed *independent* Poisson diagnostic with this arithmetic λ: **FT Over 2.5 = 52.46%; Under 2.5 = 47.54%**. This is not a fitted Dixon–Coles grid, does not account for home/away strength, injury, dispersion or game state, and **cannot** be promoted to primary `p_card` or a retroactive rank.
- A naive two-sided expected corner proxy from aggregates is `((5.63+5.56)/2)+((4.37+4.11)/2) = 9.835` corners. Under an *assumed* Poisson diagnostic, **FT Over 10.5 corners = 39.63%; Under 10.5 corners = 60.37%**. Only a sensitivity illustration: the 27-game aggregates were not all opened, per-game variance and correlation were not fitted, and corner definition/action remains unresolved.
- **1H Over/Under 0.5** remains PRIMARY_NOT_ESTIMATED: online Chinese halftime score lists were discovered but not retrieved as independently opened and reconciled complete L20 half-specific observations, and the adapter does not model 1H by merely scaling an FT λ. No invented first-half rate/primary probability.

### P2. Numeric analyst estimate — all original supplied directions

| Supplied contract direction | Analyst P(win), % | Analyst P(loss), % | Analyst P(push) under integer half-line | Mathematical expected count in that endpoint | Basis and limitations |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 1H Over 0.5 goals | 61.33 | 38.67 | 0 | 0.950 1H goals | L20 half-score proxy; independent-Poisson diagnostic, not halftime model |
| 1H Under 0.5 goals | 38.67 | 61.33 | 0 | 0.950 1H goals | Complement, assuming game runs and normal 1H action |
| FT Over 2.5 goals | 52.46 | 47.54 | 0 | 2.775 FT goals | L20 final-goal form proxy; independent Poisson, not fitted Dixon–Coles |
| FT Under 2.5 goals | 47.54 | 52.46 | 0 | 2.775 FT goals | Complement under same action convention |
| FT Under 10.5 corners | 64.45 | 35.55 | 0 | 9.833 FT corners | Empirical/team-count Poisson equal-weight *heuristic*, not fitted corner model |
| FT Over 10.5 corners | 35.55 | 64.45 | 0 | 9.833 FT corners | Complement under normal full-game corner definition |

**Meaning of “expected value”:** E[1H goals]=0.950; E[FT goals]=2.775; E[FT corners]=9.833, all **sporting-count expectations**. A different concept—financial net EV—requires an actual contract payoff/cost and legally operative void rules; **NOT_COMPUTABLE** from sporting probabilities alone. In general, for an action with win net reward `v`, loss net cost `c`, and no pushes, `EV_net = p_win * v - (1 - p_win) * c`; neither `v` nor `c` has been supplied. Market inputs and financial optimization remain outside SPORTS_ONLY / MARKET_BLIND.

**Indicative sporting expectation by side:** full-time Henan 1.275, Tianjin 1.500 goals (total 2.775; signed Henan-minus-Tianjin margin −0.225). First-half Henan 0.575, Tianjin 0.375 goals (total 0.950). Corners: Henan 5.593, Tianjin 4.241, combined 9.833. Goal and corner totals are independent *marginal diagnostics*, not a joint goal/corner model. Do not treat the expectations as modal exact scores.

**First-half rate arithmetic (L20):** Henan 15 scored/6 conceded in 20 (0.750/0.300 per game). Tianjin 9 scored/8 conceded in 20 (0.450/0.400 per game). Unfitted symmetric team/opponent strength averaging gives `lambda_1H_Henan = (0.750 + 0.400)/2 = 0.575`, `lambda_1H_Tianjin = (0.450 + 0.300)/2 = 0.375`, `lambda_1H_total = 0.950`. Poisson zero probability `exp(-0.950) = 0.38674102`, hence first-half Over 0.5 = `0.61325898`.

**Full-game rate arithmetic (L20):** Henan scores 29/20=1.450 and Tianjin concedes 22/20=1.100: `lambda_Henan=(1.450+1.100)/2=1.275`. Tianjin scores 30/20=1.500 and Henan concedes 30/20=1.500: `lambda_Tianjin=1.500`. Therefore `lambda_FT=2.775`; Poisson Under 2.5 `= exp(-2.775)*(1 + 2.775 + 2.775^2/2) = 0.47543424`; Over `=0.52456576`.

**Corner arithmetic (season 27 each):** Expected Henan corners `(152/27+150/27)/2 = 5.59259259`; Tianjin `(118/27+111/27)/2 = 4.24074074`; expected match total `= (152+150+118+111)/54 = 9.83333333`. Poisson `P(corners>=11 | lambda=9.83333333) = 0.39611831`. Observed O10.5 rates, averaged across the two teams: `(8+9)/(27+27) = 0.31481481` (events not fully independent because their earlier H2H occurs in both season samples). **Explicitly declared illustrative equal-weight smoothing**, not learnt/calibrated: `P_O10.5 = (Poisson 0.39611831 + empirical 0.31481481) / 2 = 0.35546656`, Under `= 0.64453344`. The 50/50 weight is discretionary, with **no calibrated justification**; retain both constituent estimates separately and never promote this to model p_card.

**Expected net line-count margins only (NOT financial payoffs):** E[1H goals−0.5]=+0.450 goals; E[FT goals−2.5]=+0.275 goals; E[FT corners−10.5]=−0.667 corners. These differences do NOT equal probability of success or economic value.

#### A4. Explicit analyst distribution, expected-score arithmetic, baseline and sensitivities
**Research-supported but uncalibrated full-time construction:** current UAEPL first five GF/GA home = 3/5,9/5; away =12/5,13/5. Geometric opposing strength pairing for team goals:

- `lambda_home=sqrt((Al Dhafra GF/5)*(Dubai United GA/5))=sqrt(0.6*2.6)=1.2490 goals`.
- `lambda_away=sqrt((Dubai United GF/5)*(Al Dhafra GA/5))=sqrt(2.4*1.8)=2.0785 goals`.
- `lambda_total=3.3275` goals, `expected signed Al Dhafra margin = -0.8295`. This is a *scenario* and is not the repository's fitted Dixon–Coles goal grid; goal covariance/low-score corrections are omitted. `Pois(3.32746)` total: `P(FT≥3) = 1 - exp(-lambda)*(1+lambda+lambda²/2)=0.6461`; complement `0.3539`.
- Scenario goal winner diagnostics from independent team Poissons (Skellam): Al Dhafra win ≈ 22.5%, draw ≈ 21.0%, United win ≈ 56.5%. Again NOT_PRIMARY and not a trained match-winner forecast.

**First-half totals, unsupported primary phase:** UAE league has 42 recorded 1–45 minute goals among 117 total; observed timing share `42/117 ≈ 0.3590`. Naive proportional expected 1H goals `lambda_total*(42/117)=1.1944`; choose a declared **6% analyst downward adjustment** to 1.123 (Al Dhafra no first-half goals in their 5 UAEPL matches, club-focused report `SRC-UAE014`; adjustment size deliberately heuristic and not learned). `Pois(1.123)` gives `P(1H≥1)=1-e^-1.123=0.6747`. The numerator may miss goals grouped under first-half stoppage in one provider; denominator/time bucketing and source lineage therefore remain open. This half probability is an UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO only.

**Corners, unsupported primary family:** projected home awarded `0.5*(home-for3.4+opponent-against5.2)=4.3`; projected away awarded `0.5*(away-for3.8+opponent-against3.2)=3.5`. Hence `mu_corners=7.8`. Corners are overdispersed: assumed **negative-binomial variance-to-mean ratio=1.6** (not empirically fitted), so shape `r=mu/(1.6-1)=13`, success `p=r/(r+mu)=0.625`. `P(C≤10)=NB_CDF(10;r=13,p=0.625)=0.7919`, `P(C≥11)=0.2081`. As a comparison the Poisson at the same centre yields under10.5≈0.8352, demonstrating distribution sensitivity. No independent 20-game corner ledger/variance fit exists here.

**Baseline:** `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` identical-contract executable artifact/receipt NOT_COMPUTED; 2026/27 league 3.343 gpm is a descriptive reference, NOT falsely frozen as exact-scope baseline probabilities. Expected total corners does NOT use a proved league-wide corner mean. Adjustments: managerial change, recovered injured players, home/away samples and weather remain UNFITTED, and no unverified input decides Rank1 of a PRIMARY numerical card.

| Scenario | Expected FT goals | 1H goals | Corners | P(1H Over0.5) | P(FT Over2.5) | P(Under10.5 corners) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Low-scoring/fewer corners | 2.85 | 0.98 | 6.80 | 62.5% | 54.2% | 86.8% |
| Base (published analyst diagnostic) | 3.3275 | 1.123 | 7.80 | 67.5% | 64.6% | 79.2% |
| High-scoring/higher corners | 3.75 | 1.40 | 9.20 | 75.3% | 72.3% | 66.4% |

These scenario bounds are **parameter sensitivity, not calibrated confidence intervals**; they are not reconstructed ML ensemble weights and do not demonstrate predictive skill.

#### A6. Specific risks and unadjusted comparisons
- Dubai United L20 contains 15 lower-tier fixtures; promotion/competition-strength regime prevents naive L20 blending. UAEPL current five show high-scoring games; early-season tail volatility large.
- Al Dhafra coach replacement is confirmed but managerial uplift is not estimated; returning Dubai United injured players are unnamed and may alter the odds materially.
- Exact XI, bench, late absences, field conditions and first play not confirmed. No game action should be retrospectively priced as if those were known.
- Corners source exposes only aggregate own/opponent averages for five games, not joint fixture-level covariance/variance; NB overdispersion is a transparent assumption.
- First-half source timing bins do not prove complete 1H stoppage inclusion; half-time lineup selection may matter disproportionately at a 0.5 line.
- 2025/26 head-to-head has different rosters and divisions, is not admitted as a predictive adjustment. Operator rules still open.

### A1. Exact contracts, endpoint, baseline and evidence cohort
All 6 directions: team-combined **1H regulation goals O/U 0.5**, **FT regulation (incl. stoppage; excluding ET/SO) goals O/U 2.5**, **FT regulation (incl. stoppage) awarded team corners O/U 10.5**. Conventional settled result based on integer statistic: no pushes at half-lines. Abandoned/voided/nonstandard operator treatment UNKNOWN (do not infer an operator definition). This is ISL league, no extra time, penalty shootouts excluded. No separately frozen league-only baseline; `BASELINE_P=NOT_ESTIMATED` (no admitted fully verified same-line exact league cohort). First-half and corner primary families unsupported by existing V3 soccer goal-grid adapter; contextual cycle still requires a real local release and admitted store. Source-known-at status of third-party pages not individually archived: `KNOWN_AT_UNVERIFIED` for fine-grained statistics; no pre-start clean-input certification.

**These are probabilities calculated here and now from declared human analyst parameters, NOT issued primary p_card.** All are unconditional regulation over/under pairs; W=p, L=1−p, P=V=0 under ordinary full-play convention. Numeric expected settled indicator E[1(win)]=p. Non-financial even-outcome *signed correctness diagnostic* E[win−loss]=2p−1 (not profit, not stakes or monetary EV); direction-sensitive.

First-half and corner models are both **not fitted**; their nominal precision is computational, not epistemic.

### A4. Distribution recipe; calculation, football mechanisms, baseline
**Explicit analyst fit substitute (not an ML run):** prior league 13/13 rates Delhi attack 13/13=1.000, defence 17/13=1.308; Mohun attack 23/13=1.769, defence 9/13=0.692. Unadjusted league strength cross pairing sqrt(1.000×0.692)=**0.832** Delhi goals; sqrt(1.769×1.308)=**1.521** Mohun. Qualitative home adjustment, new-player/regime and one 2026/27 league opener support a **declared centre** λ_D=**0.95**, λ_M=**1.63**; these increases are **analyst judgement**, NOT fitted coefficients. FT total Pois(2.58), with Poisson team goals independent (covariance zero by assumption, not learned); includes 0–0 states; tail retained through direct Poisson CDF (no manual truncation). Draw generated via Skellam; no Dixon-Coles fitted low-score correction, no false claim this is repository grid.

**1H heuristic:** λ_1H=1.05 (assumed 1.05/2.58=40.70% share). League HT evidence: Delhi prior-season HT rows/first-half 0.38 GF and 0.85 GA per game from [footymetrics](https://www.footymetrics.com/teams/11058825-sporting-delhi); exact both sides L20 halftime data/phase-conditioned count fit remain missing. 1H Pois(1.05) for comparison only; not derived as a trained phase model.

**Corners empirical:** Delhi 2025/26 ISL won **3.85**, conceded **5.69** per game, 9.54 combined [footymetrics](https://www.footymetrics.com/teams/11058825-sporting-delhi); Mohun 13-game corners **121=9.31 per game** [statof](https://statof.com/stats/indian-super-league/corners), and total match corners **12.85 per game** [APWin](https://www.apwin.com/team/sporting-delhi/corners/) indicating approximately 3.54 conceded **IF the provider populations and definitions match**; that join is **not independently certified**. Approx Delhi corners (3.85+3.54)/2=3.695; Mohun (9.31+5.69)/2=7.50; combined 11.195 rounded to **11.20**. Fit **negative-binomial with fixed assumed dispersion k=10** to account for overdispersion: mean=11.20, variance=11.20+11.20²/10=23.744, P(N≤10)=NB.cdf(10,k=10,p=10/(10+11.20)). This corner k is NOT estimated from real corner-level variance. Note one 2026/27 match: Bagan 8 for/4 against [performanceodds](https://www.performanceodds.com/team_stats_overall/17964/688913/); Delhi opener 1 corner vs Bengaluru 7 [footystats](https://footystats.org/india/bengaluru-fc-vs-sporting-delhi-h2h-stats). Tiny one-match samples do not override prior 13-game rates.

**Direct probability expressions** with no push half-lines: P(1H Over0.5)=1−exp(−1.05); P(FT Over2.5)=1−exp(−2.58)[1+2.58+2.58²/2]; P(Corners Under10.5)=Σ_n=0..10 NB(n;k=10,μ=11.20). Complementary directions are exactly 1−p; no odds or betting-model probabilities imported.

### A5. Selection, alternative slate and joint failure
- Priced universe: **six original supplied exact direction contracts**; no derived line-search universe was supported/frozen here, and no model-generated additional prices. No probabilities were tuned by threshold fishing or clipped at 0.90.
- Analyst sorted order A2; `RANK1_PRIMARY_GATE=NOT_ASSESSABLE`; `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1=NOT_EXECUTED`; baseline reason: no cutoff-verified full exact-league same-line cohort. `RET03, RET04, SHADOW_RANK_DELTA, SHADOW_BASE_ONLY=NOT_CALCULATED`; original p_card none.
- Conditional *analyst-only* joint failure for nominal analyst top pair (1H over 0.5 and FT under 2.5), assuming Poisson independent increments with λ_1H=1.05, λ_2H=1.53: P(1H=0 ∧ FT≥3)=exp(−1.05)×[1−PoisCDF(2;1.53)] = **6.95%**. Both-decisive = unconditional for integer half-lines if complete match; theoretical Fréchet loss bounds = [max(0,0.3499+0.4765−1), min(0.3499,0.4765)]; **joint distribution is a heuristic**, not frozen learned model; not a predictive skill statement.
- Sensitivity is not an uncertainty confidence interval and cannot manufacture a numeric PASS or certified p.

- `python -m ml capability` executed from sandbox; **exit 1**; stderr `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python: No module named ml`. Command `git ls-remote https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research.git HEAD` executed; **exit 128**, stderr `Could not resolve host: github.com`. GitHub connector can read verified pinned code/control files, **but is not an executable checkout**. Consequently cannot install `ml/requirements.txt`, run `python -m ml.workflow research`, emit a verified `EXECUTION.csv`, training/release/shadow, p_card, baseline or learned Hedge/RL weights. No runtime result claimed.
- GitHub CYCLE-v1 contextual soccer adapter would require frozen exact ISL release, at least 30 accepted ISL events, both participants seen in point-in-time store, approved features; same six supplied families not all model-supported. Soccer historical V3 fitted Dixon-Coles 90m goal grid cannot generate legitimate 1H or corner p merely from FT parameters. This file is therefore **RESEARCH_ONLY_NO_MODEL**.
- Current release uses historical data, models and online expert aggregation; **no independently executed reinforcement-learning agent** is asserted for this card. Supervised ML/RL is not inferred merely from repository implementation.

### A4. Parameter recipe / actual arithmetic / no primary ML

1. Reproduced in local Python `ALGERIA_analysis_calc.py`, with numerical JSON receipt, dated-history audit CSV; zero market features. The notebook/code is an *analyst calculator*, not the repository model.
2. Explicit effective-prior shrinkage for 2026–27 form (heuristic, **not fitted**): 8 pseudo-games at 2025–26 league scoring/conceding rate, plus four actual 2026–27 games, divided by 12. Thus Akbou attack `(4+8*33/29)/12`=1.0920, concede `(5+8*30/29)/12`=1.1063; Ben Aknoun attack `(6+8*40/29)/12`=1.4195, concede `(5+8*38/29)/12`=1.2902. **8** was chosen for analyst smoothing, not empirically learned, and is not a repository league baseline/admitted model input.
3. Regulation matchup goal intensities `λ_A=(Akbou attack + Ben Aknoun conceded)/2`=1.191092 and `λ_B=(Ben Aknoun attack+Akbou conceded)/2`=1.262931; FT λ=2.454023. Independent Poisson full-goal counts; **no fitted Dixon–Coles low-score correction**, dependence, covariance, venue adjustment, fitted xG/shot or lineup features. Winner masses and full-goal total share *one* common grid; draw includes 0–0,1–1,2–2 etc. Higher score tail from k=0..22 excluded mass <1e-12 at these intensities.
4. Half-time λ = `0.30*(last-four each team matched HT total 6/8=0.75)+0.70*(0.42*FT λ)` = 0.946483. Fraction **0.42** and weight **0.30** are unfitted scenario assumptions, not independently calibrated Algerian league parameters. `P(1H Over 0.5)=1−e^(−λ_HT)`; Under complement.
5. Full-time `P(Under 2.5)=e^(−λ_FT)*(1+λ_FT+λ_FT²/2)`; Over complement. The full-time and first-half quantities are jointly compatible with independent later-period Poisson intensity λ_2H=1.507540, conditional on an unvalidated temporal split.
6. Matchup corners: `λ_A_c=(Akbou won 5.50 + Ben conceded 8.00)/2` =6.75; `λ_B_c=(Ben won 4.00 + Akbou conceded 4.75)/2`=4.375; total λ=11.125. For a deliberately overdispersed **negative binomial r=12** (variance `μ+μ²/12`), `P(Corners ≤10) = sum(k=0..10) Γ(k+12)/(Γ(12)k!) (12/(12+μ))^12 (μ/(12+μ))^k` = 0.4869; Over complement. r=12 is analyst-assumed, **not fitted**; source samples n=4 each, overlapping league opposition strengths not adjusted.
7. Arithmetic machine precision ε_calc below ~0.00001; **sporting parameter uncertainty dwarfs numerical precision**. No `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` released; no actual verified comparison cohort, source n/parameters, fitted weights η, expert loss ledgers, cutoff hash, same-line baseline shadow or trusted execution bundle; `BASELINE_P NOT_ESTIMATED` for **all 6 supplied rows**. Do not copy external tip/model numbers or retrofit baseline 0.5.
8. Actual pinned runtime attempt: `python -m ml capability --out /mnt/data/ALGERIA_CAPABILITY_RECEIPT.csv` **exit 1** stderr `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python: No module named ml`; attempted `git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research.git /mnt/data/ALGERIA_REPO_CHECKOUT` **exit 128**, stderr `Could not resolve host: github.com`. No checkout means `ml/requirements.txt` not locally available to install, and no `python -m ml.workflow research`, `ml primary predict`, or `ml.workflow verify` could execute. The repository itself was READ using the connected GitHub API. `EXECUTION.csv`, hashes, mixture weights, learned parameters, fitted probabilities, and feedback: **NONE / NOT_RUN**. No RL agent training claimed (online expert-weight adaptation is the relevant admitted learning mechanism only when executed).

### A1. Identity, rule and endpoint interpretation
2026-27 Liiga regular season: 3×20 minute regulation; ties proceed to overtime and potentially shootout. Full-match moneyline **assumed** to include OT/SO, while a regulation 3-way result is a different contract. HPK +1.5 is assumed to use the officially recorded full-match goal margin including the single OT/SO deciding goal. Over/Under 5.5 assumes officially recorded full-match goals including the OT/SO decider, and a shootout contributes one official winning goal. **Operator-specific wording was not supplied and remains UNKNOWN_DEFINITION.** In the central idealised model, a tied 0, 2 or 4 regulation-goal game becomes 1, 3 or 5 full-match goals after a decider; consequently the 5.5 half-line classification is invariant, but other thresholds need separate handling. A 60-minute-only total and +1.5 are separately retained as sensitivity readings; no change at these half-lines under this single-goal-decider simplifying assumption. No push at integer-count half-lines. Return/void/postponement provisions unknown.

### A4. Distribution construction, baseline and expected sporting quantities
The primary GitHub `MODEL_ASSISTED_CYCLE_V1` adapter was **not executed**: `python -m ml capability --out CAPABILITY_RECEIPT.csv` exited 1 (`No module named ml`); repository `git clone` exited 128 (`Could not resolve host: github.com`). A GitHub connector successfully read pinned published rules but supplied no runnable checkout. No real fitted dataset/release, expert mixture, feedback or model bundle exists in this mini; baseline `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` NOT_EXECUTED (no source-reviewed identical-contract cohort).
**Independent analyst calculation (scenario, not a GitHub model):** independent Poisson 60-minute regulation goals. `λ_HPK = sqrt(HPK_GF_2026/13 × ILVES_GA_2026/12) = sqrt((26/13) × (24/12)) = 2.000`; `λ_ILVES = sqrt(ILVES_GF_2026/12 × HPK_GA_2026/13) = sqrt((35/12) × (37/13)) = 2.881`. Skellam difference (HPK−Ilves) and Poisson sum; both tails numerically computed using SciPy; exact one-goal OT/SO decider added to tied regulation states. No empirical home-ice, back-to-back, goalie, xG, pace, PP/PK, pulled-goalie or empty-net coefficient was fitted; these remain explicit sensitivity/qualitative limitations.
- **Expected 60-minute goals:** HPK 2.000; Ilves 2.881; combined 4.881; expected regulation margin Ilves +0.881.
- **Expected recorded full-match goals under a single goal in every OT/SO decision:** 5.054 = 4.881+P(60m tied) 0.173. Hypothetical 55% Ilves tiebreak split gives Ilves 2.976 and HPK 2.078 expected displayed goals. OT timing/type and the operator’s counting rules are not independently established.
- **Exact supplied-line probabilities:** `P(Under5.5)=PoissonCDF(k=5, λ=4.881)=63.68%`; `P(Over5.5)=36.32%`; `P(HPK +1.5)=P(HPK_reg−Ilves_reg ≥ −1)=61.96%`; `P(Ilves ML)=P(Ilves_reg>HPK_reg)+0.55×P(tie)=66.03%`. No pushes at half-goals.
Error due to Poisson numerical truncation in SciPy negligible compared with data/model uncertainty (~1e-10); **this is not a calibration error bound**. No p_card exists to satisfy numerical gating or warm/feedback learning requirements. Historic form windows overlap.

### A5. Selection, dependence and contract definitions
- Descriptive analyst order: Ilves ML 66.03%, Under5.5 63.68%, HPK+1.5 61.96%, Over5.5 36.32%. This is **not** a frozen or eligible PRIMARY_V3 card. Both opposite 5.5 totals retained but never selected simultaneously. Every supplied item assessed, no market-based candidate construction.
- Under/Over 5.5 are exact complements with no push under the stated endpoint. Ilves ML loss and HPK+1.5 loss are **mechanically disjoint** in an ordinary one-goal OT/SO decision: a defeated Ilves implies HPK +1.5 wins; an Ilves victory by ≥2 means Ilves ML wins. Not an empirical skill guarantee.
- Under the common independent-Poisson scenario, the exact joint probability that **both** analyst first and second rows lose (Ilves ML loses AND Under 5.5 loses) is 11.13%, evaluating every integer 60-minute score state and assigning HPK a 45% win share on regulation ties. No independence product was used; OT/SO counting convention assumed.
- Original proper baselines, opposite-family shadow, best non-complementary gate across expanded candidate universe, RET03, RET04, SHADOW_RANK_DELTA, SHADOW_BASE_ONLY and minimum-joint-failure freeze were NOT_EXECUTED without the primary bundle. This audit is a scenario comparison, not scored ML prediction.

### A7. Sensitivity and fragility of the supplied rows
The following are **plausible, deliberately assumed ±goal-intensity cases**, not statistical confidence intervals. OT/SO Ilves 55% share held fixed; no fitted distributions or validation.
| Case (unfitted) | λ_HPK | λ_Ilves | HPK +1.5 | Ilves ML | Under 5.5 | Over 5.5 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| BASE_RAW_CURRENT_SEASON_GEOMEAN | 2.000 | 2.881 | 61.96% | 66.03% | 63.68% | 36.32% |
| LOW_TOTAL | 1.700 | 2.581 | 62.88% | 67.01% | 73.98% | 26.02% |
| HIGH_TOTAL | 2.300 | 3.181 | 61.21% | 65.20% | 53.21% | 46.79% |
| STRONG_ILVES | 1.800 | 3.181 | 53.13% | 73.62% | 61.93% | 38.07% |
| STRONG_HPK | 2.300 | 2.681 | 69.96% | 57.52% | 61.93% | 38.07% |
| REST_BACK_TO_BACK_STRESS | 1.800 | 3.031 | 55.90% | 71.67% | 64.56% | 35.44% |
| BACKUP_GOALIE_SWING | 2.250 | 3.131 | 61.32% | 65.33% | 54.94% | 45.06% |
Tiebreaker sensitivity: Ilves full-match moneyline probability increases/decreases by ~0.8656 percentage points for each ±0.05 change in conditional OT/SO win share, because P(tie)≈17.31%. Starter/empty-net uncertainty is not reducible to a confidence interval from the available data. Adjacent rank changes are ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT; do not claim a single robust top-two pair. Central predicted winner ILVES with 66.03% **scenario** full-match win, but significant model risk.

### A2. Contract table, sports expected values, formulas
Declared candidate universe EXACT four supplied directional contracts: CHA +2.5 points, MEM −2.5 points, combined OVER 229.5 points, combined UNDER 229.5 points; do not add or silently change thresholds. Sports-only, no bookmaker odds or monetary payout used. All numbers below are `UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO / NOT_PRIMARY`, **not p_card** and not trained ML/RL.

| Analyst order | Contract | Analyst win probability | Expected sporting centre | Expected margin to line | Mechanism / limitation |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 1 | Combined **Under 229.5** (full incl OT) | **64.09%** | combined **222.46** | 229.5 − E[total] = **+7.04 points** | Normal-approx total 19.5 SD; possible OT/rotation-driven tails |
| 2 | **Grizzlies −2.5** (full incl OT) | **53.66%** | MEM margin **+4.20** | E[MEM−CHA−2.5] = **+1.70 points** | High uncertainty: large last-season/preseason reversal |
| 3 | **Hornets +2.5** (full incl OT) | **46.34%** | CHA margin **−4.20** | E[CHA−MEM+2.5] = **-1.70 points** | Exact complement to MEM −2.5 under same convention |
| 4 | Combined **Over 229.5** (full incl OT) | **35.91%** | combined **222.46** | E[total−229.5] = **-7.04 points** | Exact complement to Under 229.5 |

- **Expected full-time score:** CHA 109.13 – MEM 113.33; expected full-game total **222.46**; expected MEM margin **+4.20**. These are NOT money-profits; no financial ROI/EV can be computed without payout and terms. No value claim.
- Model inputs were fixed BEFORE computing contract tails, with no opportunity to choose the line. Same point-count object produces the four opposite probabilities; `P(CHA+2.5) + P(MEM−2.5) = 1` and `P(Over229.5) + P(Under229.5)=1`; integer scoring means no push at half-point lines in the chosen full-game endpoint.
- Uncalibrated joint object: *approximate* bivariate-normal on full-game total T and MEM minus CHA margin D. Marginal means 222.46324, 4.19853; assumed `σ_T=19.5`, `σ_D=18.5` points, zero T/D correlation for illustration only. Under wins when T≤229; MEM −2.5 wins when D≥3. `Phi((229.5−E[T])/σ_T)` and `Phi((E[D]−2.5)/σ_D)` approximate the probabilities using continuity at the half-point threshold. Assumed SDs not fitted/calibrated; team/pace, injuries and pregame lineup uncertainty explicitly not fully learned.
- Raw recent-preseason pairing: expected CHA =(96.0 + 115.0)/2 = 105.5000; expected MEM =(118.0 + 109.3333)/2 = 113.6667; prior 17-game comparative pairing CHA =123.0294, MEM =109.3235. Central means = `0.75 * preseason + 0.25 * prior17` ±0.75 points per team for home advantage (declared heuristic, not measured here). Each previous-season row is a diagnostic of a DIFFERENT rotation and season; 75/25 is discretionary sensitivity, not fitted optimal weight.
- Probabilities are scenario estimates. **Primary model p_card: NOT_ESTIMATED** for every supplied row. ML model receipt, fitted family, fitted mixture experts, η, feedback warm start, BASELINE_P, qualification: NONE / NOT_RUN. Analyst probabilities must never be substituted into primary p_card / frozen expert weights.

### A4 — Distribution construction and goal process, special teams, game rules

1. Regulation is three 20-minute periods, score tie possible after 60m. NHL regular season 5-minute 3v3 OT and shootout if needed; puck line and moneyline full-match resolution depend on named provider/operator. Shootout deciding goal convention is material at 6.5 totals. NHL final scores conventionally count one shootout deciding goal, not one per scored attempt; assumed for central contract.
2. **Analyst-only recipe** defined before inspecting target outcome: combined player-team goal strength 35% regulation 2026–27 seven-game GF/GA rate and 65% published last 82-match 2025–26 official regular-season GF/GA rate. Previous-season official totals include occasional OT goals, not entirely regulation-only; this produces an explicitly imperfect proxy requiring tested correction later. Combined offense `PIT=3.44878`, `COL=3.91220`; combined defense conceded `PIT=3.09512`, `COL=2.61159`.
3. Cross-match independent-Poisson means `λ_PIT = sqrt(PIT offense × COL defense) − 0.14 = 2.861131`; `λ_COL = sqrt(COL offense × PIT defense) + 0.14 = 3.619759`. Home +/-0.14 goals is declared judgement, NOT fitted or validated. No exact NHL proper xG/team form learned adjustment. Current goalie reports motivate scenario checks, not invisible centre changes. Goalie small-sample SV% PIT Šilovs .885, COL Blackwood .879; regression, shot-quality uncertainty, special teams, empty-net effects remain materially unfit. Source scoring table notes league 3.10 goals/team/game, but this is a diagnostic only. Historical L20 is diagnostic; stage and old roster differences do not shift the centre as if independently trainable.
4. Exact joint regulation score grid independent Poisson, states x,y from 0..30 each (scipy), normalisation residual `-2.22e-16` floating point. Regulation COL win `53.82%`, PIT win `30.81%`, draw `15.36%`. Tie branch `q=0.54` COL wins in OT/SO, **unsupported assumed parameter**, not trained. A tie gets exactly one extra final tally for the winner in full match; cumulative expected final total `λ_pit+λ_col+P(reg draw)=6.634520`. Nothing added on regulation decisive matches, which ignores empty-net special goal-tail dependence.
5. Diagnostics needed for final qualification: shot-quality/xG and independent shot source, PK/PP chance state and penalty intensities, fatigue/travel, confirmed dressed roster and goalies, in-game pulled goalie/tail, goal covariance and Skellam misspecification, retrospective reliability and frozen same-line proper scoring. Their absences limit certainty; they do not justify assuming neutral impacts.
6. **No actual primary run:** `python -m ml capability` EXIT 1 `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python: No module named ml`; `git ls-remote https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research.git HEAD` EXIT 128 DNS resolution `github.com`. Pin exists via GitHub read connector but not executable checkout. Therefore no actual `python -m ml.workflow research`, mixture weights, training files/η/feedback ledger, success receipt, or frozen prediction. `ML_NOT_RUN / RESEARCH_ONLY_NO_MODEL`, `primary p_card=NOT_ESTIMATED`. RL here is online expert weighting in available code, not an actively executed control agent. The CLI errors are in `ML_EXECUTION_ATTEMPT.json` and manifest; never claim trained performance.

### A5 — Exact contract arithmetic / W/P/L/V and sensitivity

For independent `X~Poisson(2.861131)`, `Y~Poisson(3.619759)` before any overtime, `P(COL_ML)=P(Y>X)+0.54×P(Y=X)=0.62120617`. `P(PIT+1.5)=P(Y-X≤1)=0.62049841`. `P(U6.5, full)` counts `X+Y+1_{X=Y}≤6` = `0.48224916`, which excludes 3–3 ties. Over is complement `0.51775084`. Regulation-only under = 0.529535416 and over = 0.470464584. All rows have 0 push/void in defined half-goal scenario; `p_card_analyst=W/(W+L)=W`, but **not** a model card value. No market payout evidence, therefore financial EV NOT_ESTIMATED.

### A4. Model attempts and expected score distribution
Central **UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO / NOT_PRIMARY** (no executable fitted model). Deterministic recipe frozen before probability calculation:
- POR NBA-preseason matchup = (116.50 team scored +104.00 GSW allowed)/2 = **110.25**.
- GSW NBA-preseason matchup = (111.00 team scored +108.00 POR allowed)/2 = **109.50**.
- POR league-prior comparison = (115.5 POR 2025–26 scored +115.2 GSW 2025–26 allowed)/2 = **115.35**.
- GSW league-prior comparison = (114.6 GSW scored +115.8 POR allowed)/2 = **115.20**.
- Team expected final scores: **POR 0.60 × 110.25 + 0.40 × 115.35 − 1.50 assumed away adjustment = 110.79**; **GSW 0.60 × 109.50 + 0.40 × 115.20 + 1.50 home adjustment = 113.28**. The home ±1.50 is a *mean margin 3.0* total home-relative shift and is not fitted/venue-specific.
- μ_total = 224.07; μ_PORminusGSW = -2.49. Assume total SD **19.00 points** and margin SD **17.00 points** (heuristic; no fitting), normal tails, correlation between total and margin **not fitted**, full-game including OT. Sum and difference are treated as separate marginals consistent in their means; **no validated common joint scoring grid**.
- `p(GSW +1.5)=Φ((1.5−μ_PORminusGSW)/17)=0.59278149`; `p(POR −1.5)=1−that=0.40721851`.
- `p(Under 227.5)=Φ((227.5−μ_total)/19)=0.57163030`; `p(Over 227.5)=1−that=0.42836970`. Half-point lines have zero pushes under normal expected scoreboard and no void branch known.
- Machine roundoff ε substantially below 0.001, **structural/sampling uncertainty much larger**. No Monte Carlo claims, no correlations, fitted possession/shot, player-minute model, shot-inferred pace, overtime frequency, feedback losses or learned expert mixture. Not a model receipt. More precise display digits do not imply precision of the sporting assumptions. No immutable league-only `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` frozen; `BASELINE_P` MISSING, reason `NO_LEAGUE_PROFILE` for each supplied row (0/4). NO official banked ML/RL expected value.
- Exact `ml` attempt: `python -m ml capability --out CAPABILITY_RECEIPT.csv` returns `No module named ml`; `git ls-remote` cannot resolve github.com in local worker. Further `ml.workflow research`, `verify`, `ml ingest ... --dry-run` NOT_RUN because pinned checkout/model release not present. Captured in `MODEL_RUNTIME_ATTEMPT.json`; no invented EXECUTION.csv, expert weights, η, release/ledger/shadow or calibration.

No protective joint-loss skill claim without a measured joint object.

Chase Center home GSW. Both 48m NBA exhibition context; less consistent rotations/motivation than regular season. Prior Oct 7 123–118 is same opponents but different venue, rosters/minutes not necessarily repeated. Last five GSW totals 205, 222, 241, 217, 190 (chronological reversed here: Oct4→Oct13). Scoring-environment mean around 224.1 is below line 227.5 but SD assumption high; high pace or garbage-time bench scoring easily flips. GSW+1.5 preferred by *the central heuristic only* and is far from proven edge. No financial EV because payout unspecified; if hypothetical decimal odds `d`, unit-stake cash EV = `p_win × (d−1) − (1−p_win)` (no pushes/voids) and fair break-even `d=1/p`, but **no odds were read, sought or used in model**.

**Analyst scalar process, deterministic arithmetic `UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO / NOT_PRIMARY`:**

- Start from 2026 *peer-Test* pooled attack/defence means. Australia against NZ-peer attack {AU PF n=6} = 28.833, New Zealand against peers defence {NZ PA n=7} = 27.571; AU matchup base = arithmetic mean **28.202**. NZ peer attack = 33.571, AU peer defence = 35.333; NZ matchup base **34.452**.
- Matchup/home regime assumes +/- 1.50 point move (AU +1.50, NZ −1.50), then 75% matchup/home and 25% the 3 historical H2H team means AU **19.00**, NZ **37.00**. Thereafter AU -1.50 for named flyhalf change uncertainty and NZ +0; no additional post-publication Jordie adjustment in central. **Every weight and adjustment is declared a sensitivity assumption, not a learned or validated model**; the three H2H events also occur within L20 and are deliberately an overlay on peer rates, not three new independent observations.
- Therefore E[AUS] = 0.75*(28.20238+1.50)+0.25*19−1.50 = **25.52679**; E[NZ] = 0.75*(34.45238−1.50)+0.25*37 = **33.96429**. E[total] = **59.49107**; E[NZ−AUS] = **8.43750**.
- Define latent full-game margin `M=NZ−AUS ~ Normal(8.43750, 15^2)` and total `T=NZ+AUS ~ Normal(59.49107,16^2)`, with cov(T,M)=0 **for analyst illustration only**. Team scores are continuous approximations; actual World Rugby scores are discrete and subject to tries/kicks, pitch/lineups and dependence. Historical L20 marginal variation AU margin SD 19.1, NZ margin SD 16.7; AU total SD 13.7, NZ total SD 10.9; SD margin=15 and SD total=16 are research-informed judgement calls, **not fitted out-of-sample dispersions**. Expected play does not use live target score.
- For half-point lines, no integer push. `P(AUS+9.5)=Phi((9.5−E[M])/15)=0.52823480`; `P(NZ−9.5)=1−that=0.47176520`. `P(Under 60.5)=Phi((60.5−E[T])/16)=0.52513985`; Over = complement **0.47486015**. Computed using Python `math.erf`; floating approximation error ε from numerical CDF effectively negligible, but structural model uncertainty dominant (>0.01), so gate resolution NOT_CERTIFIED.
- **Expected contract value in POINTS (not financial EV):** Australia +9.5 residual `9.5−E[NZ−AUS]` = **+1.063 points**; NZ −9.5 residual = **-1.063**. Under 60.5 residual `60.5−E[T]` = **+1.009 points**; Over = **-1.009**. These expected signed distances are **not** payoff or monetary EV; without an operator's payout/price, monetary EV is UNAVAILABLE and SPORTS_ONLY forbids injecting odds.
- Arithmetic reproduction: `CALCULATION_RECEIPT.json`, `SCENARIOS.csv`, generator source `build_bledisloe.py` (standalone), 40-game input CSV.

## A4. Analyst model and quantitative calculations — exactly four supplied directions

**Execution class:** `UNCALIBRATED_ANALYST_SCENARIO / NOT_PRIMARY`; research-only numerical calculation in Python/scipy. This is not the GitHub `MODEL_ASSISTED_CYCLE_V1`, `PRIMARY_HEDGE_MIX_V3`, calibrated `p_card`, qualified baseline, or an RL/online-expert weight update. Lineup/known-at and model qualification remain incomplete.

Aggregate basketball normal diagnostics, joint dependence of total and margin not experimentally verified:

```text
Current Chiba scoring centre = (Chiba 2026–27 PF/g + Hiroshima 2026–27 PA/g)/2 = 79.6905
Current Hiroshima scoring centre = (Hiroshima 2026–27 PF/g + Chiba 2026–27 PA/g)/2 = 90.5595
Prior analogous 2025–26 centre Chiba = 82.4698, Hiroshima = 84.2253
Chosen shrinkage weights = 75% current + 25% last prior, not fitted
Home margin adjustment +1.50 point for Chiba (Chiba +0.75; Hiroshima −0.75), not fitted
Expected Chiba = 0.75×current + 0.25×prior + 0.75 = 81.1353
Expected Hiroshima = 0.75×current + 0.25×prior − 0.75 = 88.2260
μ_T = Chiba + Hiroshima = 169.3613; μ_D = Hiroshima − Chiba = 7.0907
Assumed total σ=17.0 and margin σ=15.5 points, approximate normal CDF with continuity thresholds 171.5 and 7.5
P(Hiroshima −7.5) = Φ((μ_D − 7.5)/15.5) = 0.489465528
P(Chiba +7.5) = 1 − P(Hiroshima −7.5) = 0.510534472
P(Under 171.5) = Φ((171.5 − μ_T)/17.0) = 0.550058052
P(Over 171.5) = 1 − P(Under) = 0.449941948
P(Hiroshima win) ≈ Φ(μ_D/15.5) = 0.676330457 (ignores exact tie/OT dynamics)
```

The four rows are all the supplied directions. They are not four independent selections: each plus/minus pair and each Over/Under pair is mutually exclusive. No extra contract was invented. Predictive distribution is **unvalidated**, not a learned model.

## A5. Expected sporting quantities and selection gate

- Expected score **Chiba 81.14 – Hiroshima 88.23**, combined **169.36 points**. Expected margin **Hiroshima +7.09**; indicative winner **Hiroshima ~67.63%** with the qualification on tie/OT.
- Expected handicap-margin cushion: Chiba +7.5 = `7.5 − μ_D = +0.41` points; Hiroshima −7.5 = `μ_D − 7.5 = -0.41` points. Total line: Under 171.5 = `171.5 − μ_T = +2.14` points; Over 171.5 = `μ_T − 171.5 = -2.14` points. These are *expected sporting distances*, not expected financial profit.
- **Monetary expected value: NOT_COMPUTABLE** without a contract payout and explicit market/operator terms. No odds/market inputs considered under SPORTS_ONLY/MARKET_BLIND.
- **Numeric gating:** provisional SEL-v3 rank-1 floor 0.62 / 0.04 lead and rank-2 floor 0.58; highest analyst estimate only 0.550058 with lead 0.039524 over second different-quantity option (Under vs Chiba +7.5). Both floors fail, and no verified `p_card` exists to test primary gate. `RESEARCH_ONLY_NOT_CERTIFIED / NO_CERTIFIED_RANK1`; no calibrated slate, T2, or baseline certification.
- Joint-loss of hypothetical top-two Under and Chiba +7.5 requires their joint distribution; simple marginal Frechet bounds are **0%–44.99%**, far too wide to certify the provisional joint-loss constraint. Do not infer 0% from complementary selections.

## A6. Dependence, opponent/lineup and sensitivity routes

Predicted total and scoring margin depend on common possessions, offensive rebounds, shooting conversion, foul-rate and overtime; independence between these two was not established. Current Hiroshima schedule includes Saga twice and Kobe twice; 6–0 alone is not a calibrated rating. Chiba's early matchups include Gunma twice; raw early win-loss distributions have unequal opposition. Back-to-back/rest effect minimal from published dates but scoring/lineup unverified. 2025–26 B1 observations reflect changed squads and do not qualify as independently admitted 2026–27 Premier training.

|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| central | 81.1–88.2 | 169.4 | 51.1% | 48.9% | 55.0% |
| current_only | 80.4–89.8 | 170.2 | 45.2% | 54.8% | 52.9% |

These are conditional sensitivity cases, not posterior intervals. A 7-point change in combined scoring centre swings Under 171.5 materially. A 5-point hypothetical Hiroshima scratch/rotation loss (not an observed absence) shows how unsupported late-player information could change the spread decision. Do not assert the scratch happened.

### A4 — Actual computations and expected score
**Model status:** `ml.chat` wrapper *exists in current published code*, but `CURRENT_RELEASES.csv` at pinned SHA has ONLY `Baseball/MLB` and `Ice Hockey/NHL`, and there is no `Basketball/NBL` frozen active cycle release. The required owner-generated runtime pack is not mounted in the sandbox. Consequently the source-verified GitHub primary model and online Hedge expert/RL weights **did not execute**. No p_card, baseline_p or calibrated accuracy; no fabricated execution receipts. Real attempts: `python -m ml capability` exit 1, stderr `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python: No module named ml`; `python -m ml.chat run --help` exit 1, stderr `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python: Error while finding module specification for 'ml.chat' (ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'ml')`. This is a valid capability blocker, not a reason to stop independent research.

**Analyst scenario, explicit formula:** for current seven-game means \(F^{cur}, A^{cur}\), prior 13 (mixed phase) \(F^{old}, A^{old}\), set \(F = 0.70 F^{cur} + 0.30 F^{old}\) and identically for defence. Home mean \(\mu_{SEM}=0.5(F_{SEM}+A_{CNS})+1.25\), away mean \(\mu_{CNS}=0.5(F_{CNS}+A_{SEM})-1.25\). This +1.25/-1.25 shift is a home court ***assumption***, not a measured NBL home coefficient. Full-game final margin \(M=SEM-CNS\) approximated Normal(mean \(\mu_{SEM}-\mu_{CNS}\), SD 16); full-game total \(T\) approximated Normal(mean sum, SD 20). The SDs are rounded contextual assumptions near observed pooled last-20 standard deviations (not a fitted independent competition calibration).

- Expected Phoenix 97.8115 points; expected Cairns 91.7885; **combined expected 189.6000 points**; expected Phoenix advantage +6.0231 points.
- Analytic approximations (Phi standard normal CDF): Phoenix −4.5 = \(P(M>4.5)\) = **53.7919%**. Cairns +4.5 complement **46.2081%**. Over 195.5 = \(P(T>195.5)\) = **38.3997%**. Under complement **61.6003%**.
- Indicative straight Phoenix winner \(P(M>0)\) **64.6706%**. Winner was not supplied as a contract, so diagnostic only; tie/OT distribution not explicitly fitted.
- Expected *sporting line cushions* Phoenix −4.5 = +1.5231 points, Cairns +4.5 = -1.5231 points; Under 195.5 = +5.9000 points, Over = -5.9000 points. Monetary expected return is NOT_ESTIMATED because no payout/odds given and the SPORTS_ONLY model prohibits importing them.

### A7 — Dependence and failure pathways
- Both selections Under 195.5 and Phoenix −4.5 share a team-score process, so their results are dependent; **no jointly calibrated distribution** built from the two separate Normal marginals; independent joint-loss multiplication is NOT_VALID. Fréchet joint-loss interval for top two if assessed diagnostically, using loss shares 0.3840 and 0.4621: lower max(0, sum−1) = 0.0000, upper min(losses) = 0.3840. Bounds too wide to claim a protected top-two slate.
- Failure: sustained fast pace/high efficiency moves total above 195.5; turnover/live-ball transitions raise Cairns possessions; Phoenix short-rest fatigue changes second-half conversion; absent Hunter alters rebounding/rim defence; extra time adds points to full-match total; current opponent-adjusted season strength differs from naive GF/GA. No data-backed player adjustment fitted.

- **Source horizon:** games through 2026-10-11 for Illawarra and 2026-10-10 for Melbourne; earlier dates for injury/preview reports. Retrospective match results, match live-state, and result-based model fitting expressly excluded.
- **Venue / playing conditions:** Indoor court; weather effect not applicable. Melbourne at home, Illawarra travelling interstate. No independently quantified rest/travel penalty assigned.
- **League-specific historical benchmark:** NBL 2025–26 total mean 182.5 points, width 18.7 points, margin width 15.2, pooled home-margin effect +1.57; historical OT rate 5.5%, average OT points added 24.4 (BASE_RATES_REGISTER §7.1 / PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §4.3). The 2025–26 league-mean 91.25 points/team is the historical level; use with clear cross-season uncertainty.
- **Team form inputs:** Melbourne current n=6 PF=558 PA=539; Illawarra n=7 PF=698 PA=756. Distinct from old-season L20 comparisons.
- **Distribution object:** `MANUAL_NBL2026_NORMAL_V1`; score total T approx Normal(189.945, 21.0²), final no-tie Melbourne margin M approx Normal(+4.850, 17.0²); paired score means (T±M)/2. Dependence and covariance NOT_ESTIMATED; separate normal marginals do not establish an exact joint. Width inflation is judgemental 12% above historical residual benchmarks for early season and lineup uncertainty.
- **Pricing basis:** BASE, with low/high centre sensitivity disclosed; not a statistical confidence interval or trained mixture. The normal approximation uses historical final-score residual widths that already include overtime effects; no extra OT points are added again. Overtime branch detail is unresolved without regulation-tie and possession event data.
- **Expected points including overtime:** Melbourne 97.398; Illawarra 92.548; total 189.945; Melbourne margin +4.850. Team-specific SD, team-score covariance and any exact-score mode: NOT_COMPUTED.
- **Potential winner:** Melbourne United 0.612 / Illawarra Hawks 0.388, conditional on stated uncalibrated event distribution and a no-tie full-game endpoint.

### Decision block

**Primary direction: Under 201.5; secondary: Illawarra +10.5.** Melbourne is the more likely outright winner, but winning by eleven or more is a separate and less probable requirement. The total case is sensitive to Illawarra's volatile early-season scoring and the unknown starting fives. No betting profitability or model calibration is claimed.

| Rank among supplied propositions | Primary role | Tag | Exact contract | p_card | BASELINE_P | Distance from model centre | Main failure mechanism |
|---:|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| 1 | PICK, RANK1_UNSTABLE under sensitivity | SUPPLIED | Combined points Under 201.5 | **0.709** | 0.845 (historical league-only; stale-year comparator) | 201.5 − 189.945 = +11.555; z=+0.550 | High-possession Hawks offence, Melbourne transition scoring, late fouling/overtime |
| 2 | PICK, floor met | SUPPLIED | Illawarra Hawks +10.5 points | **0.630** | 0.722 (historical league-only) | (10.5 − 4.850)/17 = +0.332 | Hawks' league-worst defence allows a Melbourne double-digit breakaway |
| 3 | INFORMATIONAL — complement of Rank 2, NOT simultaneous pick | SUPPLIED | Melbourne United −10.5 points | 0.370 | 0.278 | z for cover=(4.850 − 10.5)/17 = −0.332 | Hawks' scoring/bench pace keeps margin within ten |
| 4 | INFORMATIONAL — complement of Rank 1, NOT simultaneous pick | SUPPLIED | Combined points Over 201.5 | 0.291 | 0.155 | 201.5 −189.945=+11.555; z=+0.550 | Melbourne defensive pressure, lower pace, Hawks inefficient shooting |

**Selection discipline.** These are all four supplied propositions in descending conditional probability, not four mutually compatible picks. The two lower-ranked rows are complements and therefore are *not* recommended alongside the corresponding higher-ranked rows. No additional player prop is fabricated to fill a four-pick slate. No line moving or margin selection based on odds.

**Rank-1 numeric gate:** arithmetic p=0.709 ≥0.620 and advantage over Rank 2 =0.709−0.630=0.079 ≥0.040; precision epsilon ≈0.002 per probability, difference epsilon ≈0.004. `PASS_BASE_CASE / RANK1_UNSTABLE_SENSITIVITY` (a +6-point total centre shift can push p below 0.62). **Rank 2:** 0.630 ≥0.580, PASS_BASE_CASE. Neither is calibrated or certified.

**Baseline coverage:** 4/4 conditional estimates from historical league-only NBL level/width; exact current-season league-rate reference not verified, marked STALE_COMPARATOR. Historical league-only baseline is not evidence of card skill. **TEAM_BASELINE_P:** the TB-1-MD centre below is used as manual starting point but has no separately calculated, independently frozen alternate output.

**Joint dependence:** under and Hawks +10.5 are not independent. Given marginals alone, joint win probability bounded by max(0,0.709+0.630−1)=0.339 and min(0.709,0.630)=0.630. Joint failure (Over 201.5 AND Melbourne −10.5) lies between 0 and min(0.291,0.370)=0.291; **NOT_COMPUTED exactly**. Avoid multiplying probabilities or claiming a protected two-leg slate.

**Team/league official pre-match evidence.** NBL official match preview dated 18 Oct gives an **expected** depth chart, not a confirmed game-selected five. Melbourne: Anthony/Ili, Machar/Wilson, Travers/Ingles, Waardenburg/Bowen and Oduro/Krslovic are depth-chart members across positions; Austin Shelley, Jai Fa'Ale and Jensen Bradtke selected to suit up per Melbourne's 17 Oct squad update (https://www.melbourneutd.com.au/news/nbl27-squad-update-round-5). Chris Goulding OUT (calf, illness delaying return to around Round 7); Sean Macdonald OUT (ACL, season). The official update is one club lineage, the NBL preview a related re-publication, not independent proof of every field.

Illawarra: NBL **expected** depth chart includes Milton Doyle, Will Hickey, Tyler Harvey, Kobe Narkle, Wani Swaka Lo Buluk, Daniel Grida, Kelan Martin, LaMarcus Days, Sam Froling, Makur Maker and other reserves. Todd Blanchfield OUT (hand, return TBC); Mason Peatling OUT (foot, season-ending surgery, original Illawarra release 9 Oct: https://www.hawks.com.au/news/mason-peatling-injury-update-nbl27). ### A4. Distribution, shrinkage, baseline and manual arithmetic

**Inputs; no code run.** Historical NBL 2025–26 league per-team mean `LM = 182.5/2 = 91.25`. Toolkit §4 NBL shrinkage `k=5`; pooled home edge `HE=+1.57`. Melbourne PF=558 PA=539 n=6; Illawarra PF=698 PA=756 n=7. Historical widths total σ=18.7, margin σ=15.2. No odds or online predictor inputs.

| Quantity | Manual expression | Result |
|---|---|---:|
| United shrunk offence `O_U` | (558 + 5×91.25)/(6+5) = 1014.25/11 | 92.205 |
| United shrunk defence `D_U` | (539 + 5×91.25)/(6+5) = 995.25/11 | 90.477 |
| Hawks shrunk offence `O_H` | (698 + 5×91.25)/(7+5) = 1154.25/12 | 96.188 |
| Hawks shrunk defence `D_H` | (756 + 5×91.25)/(7+5) = 1212.25/12 | 101.021 |
| Expected total `T` | (O_U+D_H)/2 + (O_H+D_U)/2 | 189.945 |
| Expected United margin `M` | ((O_U−D_U)−(O_H−D_H))/2 +1.57 | +4.850 |
| United expected score | (T+M)/2 | 97.398 |
| Hawks expected score | (T−M)/2 | 92.547 |
| Total σ | historical 18.7 widened by factor 21/18.7=1.123 | 21.0 |
| Margin σ | historical 15.2 widened by factor 17/15.2=1.118 | 17.0 |

**Structural limitation:** These team PF/PA per-game ratings are a transparent **proxy**, not measured possessions×ORtg/DRtg; genuine possessions, four-factor efficiency, lineup stints, team-score covariance and within-game end-period dependence were not obtained. The normal total and final-margin marginals do not define a fully coherent bivariate score grid with legal support. Probability estimates are therefore approximate and uncalibrated; exact joint and team variances NOT_COMPUTED. The width factors are analyst assumptions for incomplete 2026–27 exposure/lineups, not fitted parameters. Scores from regulation and OT are already present in historical full-game residuals: league OT rate ~5.5%, +24.4 points in OT, roughly +1.34 expected points spread across all historical games. Do **not** add +1.34 again. A separate verified pregame overtime/tie-conditional calculation is unavailable.

**Probability formulas (PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §1, half-point no push):**

- Under 201.5: `z=(201.5−189.945)/21.0=0.5502`; Φ(0.55)=0.7088 ⇒ **0.709**.
- Over 201.5: `1−0.7088=0.2912` ⇒ **0.291**.
- Illawarra +10.5: `z=(10.5−4.850)/17.0=0.3324`; Φ(0.3324)≈0.6302 ⇒ **0.630**.
- United −10.5: `1−0.6302=0.3698` ⇒ **0.370**.
- United wins full game: `z=4.850/17.0=0.2853`; Φ(0.2853)≈**0.612**; Hawks win 0.388. No final-game draw after required OTs.
- W/P/L/V per half-point row: W=`p_card`, L=`1−p_card`, P=0, V=0 **conditional on the assumed operator full-game/OT-inclusive action**. Unverified operator void rules may change action and must be resolved prior to settlement.
- Approximate numerical interpolation ε=0.002 per marginal p. Sampling/structural and input uncertainty is much larger than this tabular ε.

**Frozen league-only comparator, not fitted:** 2025–26 NBL total μ=182.5/σ=18.7 ⇒ Under 201.5 = Φ((201.5−182.5)/18.7) = Φ(1.016)=**0.845** (Over 0.155). Home-margin μ=+1.57/σ=15.2 ⇒ Hawks +10.5 = Φ((10.5−1.57)/15.2)=Φ(0.588)=**0.722** (United −10.5 0.278). These comparator parameters are from the historical league cohort, and do not represent current-season adaptation. No calibrated baseline skill claim.

**Sensitivity (alternative centres, not statistical confidence intervals):**

| Parameter scenario | Under 201.5 | Hawks +10.5 | United winner |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Baseline μT=189.945, μM=+4.850 | 0.709 | 0.630 | 0.612 |
| High-scoring +6 pts (μT=195.945); unchanged margin | Φ(5.555/21)=0.604 | 0.630 | 0.612 |
| Low-scoring −6 pts (μT=183.945); unchanged margin | Φ(17.555/21)=0.799 | 0.630 | 0.612 |
| United stronger by +4 margin pts (μM=+8.850); unchanged total | 0.709 | Φ(1.65/17)=0.539 | Φ(8.85/17)=0.699 |
| United weaker by −4 margin pts (μM=+0.850); unchanged total | 0.709 | Φ(9.65/17)=0.715 | Φ(0.85/17)=0.520 |

Genuine dependence between higher total and wider United winning margin may make both top picks fail together; no invented covariance or joint. A 2026 pace/regime revision could substantially affect Under 201.5. The current n is too small to equate recent form with persistent skill.

### A5. Supplied universe, action and exclusions

All four supplied contracts were priced, with exact proposition, half-point line, normal approximation, W/L from A4 and conditional full-game endpoint. Numeric eligible p values <=0.90. Primary selected Rank 1 Under, Rank 2 Hawks +10.5; complement rows Rank 3/4 informational. No further supported player-prop contract thresholds supplied or sourced. No derived line selected merely to inflate probability. No alternative at the same time may be represented as a distinct independent edge.

### A6. Mechanisms and adjustment register

| Adjustment or uncertainty | Applied centre change | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Venue/home | +1.57 United margin, league pooled NBL value | Historical NBL home effect per toolkit; not applied to total |
| Known Goulding/Macdonald absence | 0 additional, already captured in United 2026–27 form | Out per official club; avoid double-counting prior absence |
| Known Peatling/Blanchfield absence | 0 additional, already represented in Hawks 2026–27 mixed appearances/form | Out per official injury/preview; no verified minute-on/off coefficients |
| Potential late lineup changes | centre not shifted; widths widened | Starters not confirmed; do not invent replacements |
| Indoor weather | 0 | Not relevant |
| Rest/travel | 0 | Qualitative away travel, no validated NBL-specific quantified effect |
| Different prior-season rosters | 0 centre, old rows descriptive | 2025–26 L20 rows not transferred as equal-strength 2026–27 player regime |
| Historical-vs-2026 league pace shift | unresolved, high/low ±6 points total sensitivity | True 2026–27 league mean/possessions not independently measured |

**Mismatch-state checks:** Favourite sustains advantage => United −10.5/possibly high total; Hawks offensive response => Hawks +10.5/possibly high total; slow defensive match => Under; offensive suppression plus late fouling => Under may turn Over. No exact state weights estimated. No adjustments are falsely attributed to individual player plus-minus or a specific possession count.

### A7. Baseline/gates/selection/covariance and competing scenarios

- Primary p ordering exact to available 0.002 interpolation; complements sum 1.000 at stated precision. No draw, push or void under conditional stated full-game half-line assumptions. Overtime approximated as historical component of final scores only; exact OT accounting remains an unresolved field.
- Rank 1 p=0.709 gap=.079 (base gate pass); high-scoring scenario yields 0.604 below 0.62 threshold => RANK1_UNSTABLE_SENSITIVITY. Rank 2 base p=0.630 ≥0.58, but stronger-United scenario may fall to 0.539. Do not present numerical gates as success guarantees.
- Baseline difference (card − league-only comparator): Under 0.709−0.845=−0.136; Hawks cushion 0.630−0.722=−0.092. League-only comparator may be stale; no evidence of incremental predictive edge or calibration.
- Uncalibrated sensitivity means **not a reliable implied probability for financial or staking decisions**. No odds/expected monetary value, EV or market-adjusted inputs considered.
- `SHADOW: NOT_IN_SCOPE`, `ML/RL: NOT_RUN`, `SETTLEMENT: NOT_PERFORMED`, `CANONICAL_IMPORT: NOT_PERFORMED`.

These are **not unconditional no-result/void-inclusive masses**, because the user's operator was not provided. No in-play status or outcome has been used to grade them.

**Rank-1 gate:** p≈0.732 ≥ 0.62; next eligible non-complementary p≈0.606; lead≈0.126 ≥ 0.04; `PASS_BASE_SCENARIO`, ε≥0.002 for Φ-table precision and materially greater structural uncertainty. High-scoring sensitivity changes the order; `SCENARIO_UNSTABLE`, not a robust performance claim. **Rank-2 floor:** p≈0.606 ≥ 0.58 (`PASS_BASE`, yet not calibrated). Only two picks; the remaining two are complement rows. All four supplied rows printed without threshold shopping or invented replacement lines.

**Potential outright winner:** **Pakistan, conditional estimate approximately 0.60** (Sri Lanka approximately 0.40, excluding tie/no-result/DLS ambiguity). This is a separate, low-confidence analyst judgement from home venue, 2025 home ODI head-to-head and squads, **NOT** output from the quantified first-innings distribution, and not a calibrated win probability. Winner endpoint/tie/no-result shares NOT_COMPUTED; therefore the full three-/four-way winning state is INCOMPLETE. Confidence is lower than a 60% label may suggest.

**Decision rationale:** Pakistan's historical last-20 ODI innings averaged 237.5, but this includes shortened successful chases; this does not imply a 237.5 batting-first expectation. Sri Lanka conceded 282.2 per game across its last ten ODIs, while Pakistan posted 299/5 batting first against Sri Lanka in Rawalpindi in Nov 2025. The 311.5 total is above the conditional first-innings centre of 277.75 by 33.75. In contrast, Pakistan clearing 26.5 in five overs is a small cushion above the expected first-five run centre 29.5 and is sensitive to the unconfirmed opening batters/new-ball attack and actual strip. Both selected rows can lose together: a very slow opening followed by a later high score or a very fast opening coupled with below-312 finish has different consequences; their precise joint dependence is NOT estimated.

No `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` with verified matching 2026 batting-first first-five cohort available. Do not present 242 as 2026-specific.
- **R2 window blend for comparison:** `WEIGHTS/RECIPE_NOT_FULLY_REPRODUCED` because overlapping windows cannot be independent fitted sample rates and the current true global ODI baseline/strength-adjusted opposition schedule is missing. No invented k or derived adjusted team strengths; manual scenario-centres below are exposed judgement rather than falsely labelled R2 output. Pakistan raw L20 237.5 is not automatically an expected 50-over innings, because many innings stopped when targets were reached. Season league means, strengths and exact 5-over distributions remain MISSING.
- Historical sources and independent verification attempts are in A8. Abandoned matches, 2026 recent T20s and game-day ODI outcomes are excluded from scoring windows. Rows with exact innings overs unclear are labelled rather than manufactured. **Windows overlap; counted once.**

### A4. Conditional distribution, arithmetic, expected score and sensitivity

**Why no exact ball-by-ball model:** No verified pre-first-ball live official XI, top-four ordering, new-ball pairing, recent five-over scores with legal-ball deliveries, or pitch strip data. No historical verified venue/phase residual variance estimate. Therefore the below is a transparent, **subjective analyst approximation** with uncertain inputs, not a calibrated fit, bootstrap or true derived innings/wicket transition grid. Model widths are assumptions; the missing inputs prevent objective accuracy assessment.

**Full first-innings runs:** one conditional three-component **normal approximation** for completed 50-over-scheduled innings including all-out termination in the historical dispersion; scenario weights low/base/high = 0.25 / 0.50 / 0.25, centres μ = 255 / 278 / 300 runs, common assumed SD = 52.0 runs. Rationale: blended manually from Pakistan's recent completed innings including a 299/5 first-innings ODI versus this opponent at the venue, Sri Lanka's recent L10 conceded 282.2, historical venue mean 242; scenario range for pitch/XI uncertainty. Does **not** assert a measured 52-run residual or mathematically explicit 10-wicket state: this remains the main structural limitation. Mean = .25×255 + .50×278 + .25×300 = **277.75 runs**; width ~54.4 after between-scenario spread, not a calibrated confidence interval.

\[
P(U311.5)=\sum_{j\in\{L,B,H\}}w_j\,\Phi((311.5-\mu_j)/52)
\]

- LOW z=(311.5−255)/52=1.087; Φ≈0.861; contribution .25×.861=.2153.
- BASE z=(311.5−278)/52=0.644; Φ≈0.740; contribution .50×.740=.3700.
- HIGH z=(311.5−300)/52=0.221; Φ≈0.588; contribution .25×.588=.1470.
- **Under 311.5** ≈ .2153+.3700+.1470=**0.7323**; **Over 311.5** ≈ 1−.7323=**0.2677**. Φ interpolation ε≈.002; additional model/parameter uncertainty much larger. Half-line yields no push if the innings contract is decisive.

**Pakistan first 5 completed overs:** separate conditional three-component normal **marginal approximation**, scenario weights 0.25/0.50/0.25, centres μ_5 = 27 / 29.5 / 32 runs, common **assumed** SD=11 runs; arbitrary judgement informed by plausible early ODI scoring, **not** derived from a verified opening-30-ball sample. This quantity is intrinsically integer and nonnegative; Gaussian tail below zero is a small unnormalised approximation (base Φ(−29.5/11) about 0.004), disclosed as such. No direct link to wickets modelled. Mean=29.5 runs.

- LOW z=(27−26.5)/11=.045; P(Over26.5)=Φ(.045)≈.518; weighted .25×.518=.1295.
- BASE z=(29.5−26.5)/11=.273; P(Over26.5)=Φ(.273)≈.608; weighted .50×.608=.3040.
- HIGH z=(32−26.5)/11=.500; P(Over26.5)=Φ(.5)≈.6915; weighted .25×.6915=.1729.
- **Over 26.5** ≈ .1295+.3040+.1729=**0.6064**; **Under 26.5** ≈ **0.3936**. Rounding to three decimals: .606 and .394.

**Scenario probabilities (not 95% confidence bounds):**

| Scenario / assumption | Under 311.5 | Over 26.5 | Consequence |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Low: 255-run innings / 27 after five | 0.861 | 0.518 | Under stronger, first-five Over no longer rank-2-floor eligible |
| Base: 278-run innings / 29.5 after five | 0.740 | 0.608 | Both directions favoured |
| High: 300-run innings / 32 after five | 0.588 | 0.692 | Over five becomes Rank 1, total Under falls below Rank-1 threshold |
| Weighted primary | **0.732** | **0.606** | Scenario-dependent rankings; don't claim robust gate |

**Score/wickets expected object:** Pakistan first-innings runs 277.75, conditional full-overs-scheduled; projected five-over runs 29.5, SD 11 assumed. Wickets lost at five overs, total wickets/expected overs remaining, Sri Lanka response, run covariance, win margin and no-result probability **NOT_COMPUTED**; no falsely precise 50-over team v team score projected. The two marginal approximations don't constitute a physical monotone ball-by-ball shared grid; P(5-over result, 50-over result) joint is **unquantified**.

**Joint failure bounds, selected Rank 1 Under311.5 and Rank 2 Over26.5:** losses are innings Over311.5 p≈.268 and first-five Under26.5 p≈.394. Without a verified joint phase→innings model, Fréchet: `max(0,.268+.394−1)=0 ≤ P(both lose) ≤ min(.268,.394)=.268`. Historical diagnostic threshold 0.30/0.35 is not crossed at upper bound under these assumed marginals, but the true joint not estimated, and parameter uncertainty is omitted. Do **not** multiply as if independent.

**Baseline and EV:** Venue first-innings historical mean 242 with n=31 is contextual only, unverified source-date cutoff and mismatched competition-era; a valid matching league-only BASELINE_P is not reproducible. Five-over baseline unavailable. No market odds or stake conditions supplied; monetary EV cannot be computed and market odds are excluded under SPORTS_ONLY. Probabilities above are *not* price edges or validated advantages. `TEAM_BASELINE_P: NOT_COVERED_FOR_CRICKET`.

### A7. Statistical controls, comparisons and gates

- `P_ORDER_V2`: only four exact supplied directions priced; ranked by p_card; rank 1 and 2 are primary picks, 3 and 4 informational; no q tiers.
- **Rank-1 numerical gate:** PASS on chosen manual mixture at p .732, best independent alternative p .606, lead .126; in high-run scenario it fails for Under 311.5 and row order changes; `ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT/SCENARIO_UNSTABLE`.
- **Rank-2 floor:** PASS base at .606 but LOW scenario .518 fails. No extra confidence attributed to provider line or toss alone.
- `RET03` shadow gate (exclude same innings quantity as Rank 1): Over26.5, .606, lead .126, PASS_BASE. `RET04` alternate different quantity: same Over26.5; NOT_PRIMARY. `XCARD6` exact-joint failure optimization: NOT_COMPUTED, Fréchet interval only. `RANK_DELTA`: NONE because no frozen league-only comparator. `BASE_ONLY`: Under311.5 .740, Over26.5 .608; same rank1/2.
- `PRECISION ε`: .002 per Φ reading from official repo normal table, but true epistemic error from unvalidated μ, σ and missing innings/phase physical transition parameters is far larger; ε is not an accuracy uncertainty bound.
- `SAME-QUANTITY`: Over/Under at each threshold forced complements; no dummy Hit@2 credit, no pushes for half-run lines under a decisive endpoint.
- `PERFORMANCE_ELIGIBLE`: NO. Previous ODI examples are sourcing and contextual form, not an out-of-sample evaluation of this method.

### A4 — Distribution, formulas, expected values and line action

**Scope:** simple hand-priced **conditional normal approximation**, not a possession-fitted model and not externally validated. Exact-competition league-wide `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` unavailable: `BASELINE_P = NOT_DERIVED_EXACT_COMPETITION` for every contract. No NBA/WNBA/NBL parameters transferred. No exact quarter/overtime grid enumerated; assumed final-score distributions implicitly include overtime and require live operating-rule check. Normal continuity corrections are approximate, not exact integer score probabilities.

**Raw current-season n=5 team comparison:**

- KP points = 61.4 PF / 65.6 conceded; Žabiny B = 68.8 PF / 64.2 conceded.
- KP matched centre `(61.4 + 64.2)/2 = 62.8`; Zab centre `(68.8 + 65.6)/2 = 67.2`; **raw total 130.0, away signed margin 4.4**. This is a *raw points-matching approximation*; offensive/defensive rating per 100 possessions unavailable. League game-wide scoring and distribution parameters not derived.
- **Analyst adjustment ledger:** historical adult matchup direction +2.1 to Žabiny, local-home advantage +0.5 to KP: away margin `4.4+2.1−0.5=6.0`. Back-to-back/rest uncertainty lower total centre by 0.45, residual possibility of OT partly returns 0.25: `130.0−0.45+0.25=129.8`. These magnitudes are explicit **subjective analyst assumptions**, not measured Czech league effects. Unadjusted p values separately below.
- **Assumed outcome SDs**: full final total σ_T = **21.0 points**; signed Zab−KP margin σ_D = **16.0 points**. These widths reflect uncertainty, not independently measured league residual widths. Let T∼Normal(129.8,21²), D∼Normal(6,16²). **Assume T independent of D** as a simplifying joint assumption only; no fitted covariance. Expected KP=(T−D)/2=**61.9**; Zab=(T+D)/2=**67.9**; team marginal SD `sqrt((21²+16²)/4) ≈ 13.2`, Cov(KP,Zab)=(21²−16²)/4=46.25 points², correlation ≈0.265. These are implied mathematical parameters, not directly observed.

**Total family (139.5, final points):**

`z_U=(139.5−129.8)/21 = +0.462`. With toolkit Φ table `P(UNDER)=Φ(+0.462)≈0.678`, `P(OVER)=1−0.678=0.322`. Unconditional W/P/L/V: Under `(0.678/0/0.322/0)`, Over `(0.322/0/0.678/0)` under assumed no-void full game.

**Margin family (Zab signed D and integer final scores):**

- `D≥5` wins Zab -4.0; `D=4` pushes; `D≤3` wins KP +4.0.
- Standard continuity bins give `W_Z≈P(D>4.5)=Φ((6−4.5)/16)=Φ(0.09375)≈0.5374`.
- `Push≈P(3.5<D<4.5)=Φ((4.5−6)/16)−Φ((3.5−6)/16)=Φ(−0.09375)−Φ(−0.15625)≈0.0247`.
- `L_Z≈P(D<3.5)=Φ((3.5−6)/16)=Φ(−0.15625)≈0.4379`.
- `p_card_Z=W/(W+L)=0.5374/(0.5374+0.4379)≈0.551`; KP reversed `0.4379/(0.9753)≈0.449`. Unconditional W/P/L/V for Zab -4: `(0.5374/0.0247/0.4379/0)` and KP +4 `(0.4379/0.0247/0.5374/0)`. Conditional decisive denominator about **0.9753**. Exact push bin requires true integer model; the smoothed bin is approximation.
- Winner `P(Zab wins)=Φ(6/16)=Φ(0.375)≈0.646`, complement KP 0.354 (draw after overtime excluded). Expected win margin 6.0 for Zab; **winner is not a 64.6% calibrated market model**.
- **Arithmetic precision:** Φ table/interpolation ε≈0.002 p per row; push-bin uncertainty may be greater because no fitted discrete league margin mass or OT tie enumeration. Complements total 1 on decisive events.

**Unadjusted sensitivity anchor:** T130.0, D4.4, same assumed widths: `P(Under)=Φ((139.5−130)/21)=Φ(0.452)≈0.674`; `P(Zab-4 | decisive)` with continuous push approximation ≈0.512. Thus margin preference is adjustment-dependent and fragile; result is **not** described as empirically proven.

**Low/base/high assumptions** (not confidence intervals; fixed widths, varied scoring and roster scenarios):

| Scenario | T centre | D (Zab−KP) centre | P(Zab −4 given decisive) | P(Under 139.5) | Notes |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Low-pace / KP resilience | 122 | 3 | ≈0.475 | ≈0.797 | League scoring below current five-game blended anchor |
| Base | 129.8 | 6 | ≈0.551 | ≈0.678 | Tiny current-season sample, subjective adjustments |
| High-pace / Zab advantage | 141 | 9 | ≈0.626 | ≈0.472 | Rank1 can change; NOT stable at 62% selection floor |

**Joint dependence:** same coherent mathematical T,D independent *assumed*, but no calibrated joint or rigorous OT/int integer grid exists. Don't multiply to claim two-hit percentage. For rank1 Under (loss mass .322) and rank2 Zab -4 (loss mass .4379 unconditional, push .0247), Fréchet joint-loss range `max(0,.322+.4379−1)=0` to `min(.322,.4379)=.322`; **JOINT_UNQUANTIFIED / bounds only**. The straight Over and Under force exactly one success absent void; ±4 identical margin pair is complementary with common push.

### A5 — Baseline, candidate selection, shadow controls

- Four supplied propositions priced once on same final-score family and listed in probability rank order. Candidate universe **restricted to four supplied contracts** because no independent sourced, fixed-derived line recipe exists for other thresholds or player props. **No invented alternate totals or player lines**; no threshold tuning toward 90%.
- `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1`: `NOT_DERIVED_EXACT_COMPETITION`, because a separate full 2026–27 women's 1. liga eligible fixture cohort, mean, SD and action-endpoint convention were not compiled; 20 games per team are not this cohort. Shadow rank-delta `NONE_BASELINES`, baseline-only `NONE_LEAGUE_COMPARATOR`; alternative shadow across different quantity is rank2 Zab −4 conditional p≈.551 and fails p≥0.58.
- Rank-1 gate: candidate Under 0.678, best alternative Zab−4 0.551, margin .127 > .04, floor > .62, **numeric PASS in base case**; scenario high pace drops Under to ~0.472, **SCENARIO_UNSTABLE / ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT**. Rank2 0.551 < 0.58 **FLOOR_FAIL**, cannot call top-two compliant high-confidence slate.
- Selection-line distance: total `139.5−129.8=+9.7` or z +.462; away cover at 4 uses continuous z `(6−4.5)/16=+.09375` before push adjustment. A margin only two points above the spread is vulnerable to minute/selection shocks.

### A6 — Failure routes, contextual mechanisms and current uncertainty

1. **Unders:** unexpected pace or unusually effective three-point shooting (KP recent 5-game variability 35–93 PF; Zab 58–76), weak defensive rebounding or OT/late fouling could move the total above 139.5.
2. **Žabiny −4:** if KP's high-scoring contributors are selected while Žabiny rotates after 17-Oct travel, a 1–3 point Žabiny win or KP outright victory loses; exact 4-point margin pushes; starting fives and bench unverified.
3. **Same-city home advantage:** smaller than generic inter-city factor; magnitude of +0.5 for KP speculative.
4. **Team-name mismatch and book settlement:** user line may refer to entirely different KP Brno B squad/competition. Do not act on the four percentages without original event ID and operator settlement definition.
5. **No injuries confirmed:** no verified absence is not proof of player fitness. Attempts do not close exact-game medical data.
6. **Distribution approximation:** normal family can emit non-integer, negative, or nonphysical scores in tails and does not calculate per-possession attack/defence, exact injury scenarios, score-state dependence or OT as separate states. This may be material in a second-tier small-sample league.

These are not certified league residual SDs. Without more source evidence there is no skill or calibration claim.

**One common margin/total distribution; declared 0.25 / 0.50 / 0.25 scenario mixture:**

|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|

Scenario centres, widths and weights are explicit **subjective assumptions**, not independently estimated league parameters. The unrounded score blend 159.4375/+2.0625 is only the anchor. For each scenario take `T~Normal(μ_T,20²)` and `M~Normal(μ_M,20²)` independently (zero T–M covariance as **assumption, not finding**); then team score `Nagoya=(T+M)/2`, `Okayama=(T−M)/2`, team conditional SD `sqrt((400+400)/4)=14.14` and conditional score covariance `(400−400)/4=0`. Every line is evaluated from this same normalized mixture. Sum of mixture weights=1. Overtime is implicitly represented in historic full-game totals and width, not explicitly simulated; this is a source/model limitation.

| Scenario | Φ((μ_M+1.5)/20) Nagoya +1.5 | 1 − prior = Okayama −1.5 | Φ((161.5−μ_T)/20) Under | 1 − prior = Over | Φ(μ_M/20) Nagoya winner approximation |
