### A5. Same-line arithmetic and hand-assumed scenario pricing

**METHOD WARNING:** The supplied four contract probabilities cannot be *empirically calibrated* from the accessible sample: no verified exact-competition league centre/dispersion baseline, true season-wide opponent adjustment, target starters or independently fitted possession/covariance model. The following is **one explicitly subjective coherent normal approximation**, a sensitivity study, **not a validated forecast**. A precise `p_card` does not measure accuracy. NBL/NBA parameters are not copied into PBA. The actual PBA plays **48 minutes, 12-minute quarters, retains a 4-point shot and six-foul disqualification**, verified with 2026 PBA commissioner reports; not 40-minute FIBA or ordinary NBA without four-point zone. Historical outcomes are final including OT when stated; no separate regulation-tie/OT-point process could be verified.

**Raw matched-rate baseline from current 11 Governors' Cup games each:**

- TNT scored `1184/11 = 107.636`; Converge allowed `1224/11 = 111.273`; expected TNT `μ_TNT=(107.636+111.273)/2=109.455` points.
- Converge scored `1355/11 = 123.182`; TNT allowed `1076/11 = 97.818`; expected Converge `μ_CON=(123.182+97.818)/2=110.500` points.
- Hence **raw point-total centre** `μ_T=219.955≈220.0`, **TNT signed margin centre** `μ_M=-1.045≈-1.0`. THIS IS NOT an opponent-adjusted possessions × ORtg model and is not an accurate independent league baseline. It uses both teams' PBA rate pairs, incorporates older imports, and does not prove a true expectation. Previous season regime is not admitted to the central mean. No fake shrinkage coefficient, league SD or home edge is applied.

**Assumed uncertainty mixture (frozen before hand pricing):** low 25%, base 50%, high 25%. TNT margin means `-5,-1,+3` points, shared **σ_M=20 points assumed**; total means `212,220,228` points, shared **σ_T=24 assumed**. Shifts ±4 margin/±8 total are explicitly *analyst-defined uncertainty bounds* over import minutes/pace/opponent strength, not fitted effects. Widths are widened relative to a sharply specified single match to avoid false certainty from one Macau blowout; NOT PBA-league measured. Marginal normal draws share a baseline score centre but joint dependence, regulation/OT and covariance remain **NOT_ESTIMATED**; therefore exact joint-loss calculations are not supported. These marginals are scenario-compatible, not an empirically fitted bivariate score-object.

**Normal toolkit hand calculation** using `Φ` and basketball integer-margin threshold correction; rounding/interpolation arithmetic uncertainty ε ≈ 0.002 for each `p_card` (additional subjective model uncertainty much larger):

- **Under 221.5**: `Φ((221.5−μ_T)/24)`. Low `Φ(9.5/24=0.396)≈0.654`, base `Φ(1.5/24=0.0625)≈0.525`, high `Φ(-6.5/24=-0.271)≈0.393`. Mixture `0.25×0.654 + 0.50×0.525 + 0.25×0.393 = 0.524` (0.5243); Over `1−0.524=0.476`. No full-game integer total push at .5.
- **TNT +2.0**, score margin `M=TNT−Converge` is integer, W when `M≥−1`, P when `M=−2`, L when `M≤−3`. Normal continuity bands: `W=1−Φ((-1.5−μ_M)/20)`; `P=Φ((-1.5−μ_M)/20)−Φ((-2.5−μ_M)/20)`; `L=1−W−P`. Low (μ=-5): `W≈0.431,P≈0.020,L≈0.549`; base (μ=-1): `W≈0.510,P≈0.020,L≈0.470`; high (μ=+3): `W≈0.589,P≈0.019,L≈0.392`. Mixture unconditional `W≈0.510,P≈0.020,L≈0.470`, `p_card=W/(W+L)=0.510/(0.980)=0.520`. Opposite Converge -2.0: `W≈0.470,P≈0.020,L≈0.510`, decisive probability `0.470/0.980=0.480`. Approximation precision ties within ~0.004 to Rank 1, **PRECISION_TIE / not a meaningful ordering margin**.
- **Winner:** Conditional no-tie basketball final-margin `P(TNT win)≈Φ(μ_M/20)` with same low/base/high scenarios: `Φ(-0.25)≈0.401, Φ(-0.05)≈0.480, Φ(0.15)≈0.560`. Mixture `0.25×0.401+0.50×0.480+0.25×0.560=0.480`; Converge `0.520`. Small no-tie discretisation moves <~0.002 here; extra-time endpoint handled only implicitly through historic final-score observations. Exact OT winner/total scoring distribution missing.
- **Expected score:** conditional representative `(μ_T+μ_M)/2=(220−1)/2=109.5` TNT, `(220+1)/2=110.5` Converge; both side means add to total. Variances not independently identified: a joint covariance and team-score SD cannot be legitimately reconstructed from assumed marginal σ without joint correlation; hence `TEAM_SCORE_SD NOT_ESTIMATED`, not 0 or fabricated.
- **Sensitivities:** Under 221.5 across total scenarios 39.3%–65.4%, TNT +2.0 decisive across signed-margin scenarios ~44%–60%. These are **scenario ranges**, not statistical confidence intervals. All opposite-market rows price from same respective marginal family; complement and push integrity maintained.

**No borrowed baseline:** `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` exact PBA 2026 mean, spread/total SD, n, cohort cutoff **NOT_DERIVED**; `TEAM_BASELINE_P` `NOT_COVERED` (PBA is not the toolkit TB-1-supported NBA/WNBA/NBL cohort); same-line `BASELINE_P NOT_ESTIMATED`. No 0.500 placeholder or retro-fit. Missing confirmed starters/import minutes, stage/pace and source-known-at prohibit any suggestion of calibrated 62%+ accuracy. A league-only 2026 Group A partial sample may support a later proper benchmark after independently validated fixture/box-score assembly; not built here. No ML model, Python, fitting, shadow or RL result consulted/executed.

### A7. PBA rules, tactical match-up, motivation and winner

This is NOT 40-minute B.LEAGUE / FIBA, despite a 2026 move toward FIBA interpretations. PBA keeps **4×12-minute quarters, six personal fouls to disqualification and four-point field goals** (not all NBA-equivalent); verified June 26 2026 Philippine Star and July 9 Inquirer descriptions. Five-minute OT is typical professional basketball but an exact competition-owner 2026 written clause governing repeat OTs and the four supplied operator contracts was not independently located: `OT_DURATION_COMPETITION_VERIFICATION_OPEN / OPERATOR_OT_INCLUSION_UNKNOWN`; the analysis explicitly assumes normal completed-game OT-inclusive endpoint. 4-point shooting and high pace widen totals uncertainty, but do not alone assign a numeric over edge. Venue indoor; weather field not material.

TNT has the *rest advantage* and a restored high-level import plus deeper local availability relative to its earlier short-handed games. Converge has momentum with Clark, explosive potential from Gomez de Liaño/Arana/Baltazar and recent soft-opponent 162/152 outputs. Both have already clinched Group A quarterfinal spots and this game is seeding-sensitive; neither should be assumed to rest its first five automatically. Neither last high-scoring Macau game demonstrates a similar result against a stronger team. Previous July H2H TNT +3 preceded several import and player-regime changes; low predictive weight.

**Potential winner:** **Converge FiberXers ~52%** (small advantage) vs **TNT Tropang 5G ~48%**. **Score centre:** TNT 109.5–Converge 110.5, game total 220.0, without pretending an exact integer final-score outcome. An actual upset by TNT is fully plausible. This finding could reverse with verified Oct18 starter/minutes/rest inputs; the probability is **uncalibrated/fragile**. The complementary −2 market remains below 50% decisive because a win by just 1–2, an exact 2-point push, or TNT victory all matter.

### A5. Statistical construction, all inputs and calculations

**Official league record:** both 2W-2D-4L in eight LaLiga matches, Osasuna 8 scored /18 conceded, Racing 13 scored/23 conceded; [LaLiga fixture](https://iaas-public-front-pro.laliga.com/partido/temporada-2026-2027-laliga-ea-sports-ca-osasuna-r-racing-club-9). Separate pregame secondary providers: [FootyStats Osasuna](https://footystats.org/clubs/ca-osasuna-287) 1H GF2 GA8 in eight, 1H Over0.5 7/8; Racing analogous 1H GF10 GA12, 1H Over0.5 7/8 (collector/cross-check incomplete). [QueroStats matchup](https://querostats.com/) 2026 xG Osasuna for **1.33** against **1.57**, Racing for **1.43** against **2.14**, sample n8 each, per team; derived from incomplete third-party coverage, NOT independently audited play-by-play. Osasuna 9.5 shots and 4.0 on target; Racing 11.5 and 4.9 according to provider; no independently reconciled shot-quality per opponent. League-side nominal xG around 1.47, not an independently fitted 2026 LaLiga full-population baseline. Per [LEAGUE_PROFILES](https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/blob/3e29a58693e2ed02ff862e9bf0f751af2feadee1/LEAGUE_PROFILES.md), no admitted replacement model, `DATA_PENDING`.

**Scoring centre derivation (analyst, not fitted):** geometric attack-v-defence pairing `sqrt(1.33×2.14)=1.687` for home; `sqrt(1.43×1.57)=1.498` for away. The geometric aggregation is a declared heuristic. Contextual home/away/rest/missing-XI **assumptions**, not measured causal effects: +0.113 for Osasuna, −0.198 for Racing, yielding base λ_H=**1.80**, λ_A=**1.30**, λ_total=**3.10**. The signs reflect Osasuna's four home games (2–3) versus Racing's four road games (4–16), partly confounded by opponents. Coach/keeper availability receives **zero separately quantified mean adjustment** and scenario width instead; no double counting individual injuries. No sourced exact opponent Elo or schedule-weight correction is claimed.

**Half-specific centre:** observed 1H goals in matches involving Osasuna `(2+8)/8=1.25`; Racing `(10+12)/8=2.75`; naive midpoint `(1.25+2.75)/2=2.00`, atypically high and shared-overlap context. LaLiga early-season first-half goal benchmark ≈**1.33** (secondary 2026 competition statistics, population n unverified). Explicit analyst shrinkage fraction w=`8/(8+12)=0.40` (k=12 subjective, NOT repository-fitted), giving base λ_1H=`0.40×2.00+0.60×1.33=1.598≈1.60`. Seasonal home-only weak attacks motivate low case1.30, early-match defensive turmoil high1.80. Do NOT transfer EPL default half proportion 0.43.

**Corner inputs/definition:** [QueroStats](https://querostats.com/) indexed estimates per match Osasuna corners FOR **3.7**, AGAINST **5.0**, Racing FOR **5.4**, AGAINST **8.4** (n approximately 8, coverage possibly seven). Raw opponent matching `(3.7+8.4)/2+(5.4+5.0)/2=6.05+5.20=11.25` corners. [MatchScan](https://matchscan.net/) indexed each-team match corner total averages Osasuna ≈8.13 / Racing ≈13.13, 8 observations each; league benchmark ≈9.76. Analyst shrinkage 70% to league, base μ_corners=`9.76+0.70×(11.25−9.76)=10.803≈10.80`. This is not independently corroborated exact-game ESPN/Opta `wonCorners`, nor a validated count/dispersion distribution. Model `Poisson(μ_corners)` is a *diagnostic approximation* that may be too narrow; **corners NOT FORMALLY ELIGIBLE** until named provider, native event and source fields reconciled. Zero invented verified provider outputs.

**Frozen joint scenario design before pricing (weights .25 / .50 / .25):**

| Scenario | Weight | Osasuna full λ | Racing full λ | FT λ | First-half λ | Second-half λ = FT−1H | Corners mean | Basis |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| LOW | .25 | 1.60 | 1.10 | 2.70 | 1.30 | 1.40 | 9.70 | Osasuna home caution / new regime |
| BASE | .50 | 1.80 | 1.30 | 3.10 | 1.60 | 1.50 | 10.80 | Current-season xG pairing + shrunk half/corners |
| HIGH | .25 | 2.05 | 1.55 | 3.60 | 1.80 | 1.80 | 11.90 | Defensive errors / away vulnerability |

Each scenario models independent home/away full-time Poisson goals and `1H~Poisson(λ_1H)`, `2H~Poisson(λ_FT−λ_1H)`, with half team allocation not separately fitted; this specifies a *consistent total-goals marginal/phase model*, but **not a fully verified team-by-phase/goal-corner bivariate model**. Full match home/draw/away uses the identical scenario goal λs, no arbitrary winner probability insertion. Post-injury roster and state dependence missing; low-score Dixon–Coles adjustment not fitted. These limitations prevent calibration claims.

**Goal calculations with full steps (PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §2):**

Weighted mix HOME `.25×.490+.5×.492+.25×.494=.492`; DRAW `.25×.249+.5×.230+.25×.213=.2305≈.230`; AWAY `1−home−draw≈.278`; three states sum1 by construction. No extra-time or penalty winner in LaLiga.

Rounded LOW at9.7 ≈`.379`, BASE10.8 ≈`.516`, HIGH11.9 ≈`.641`; weighted `.25×.379+.5×.516+.25×.641≈.513`. O/U sum exactly1 by complementary definition; UNDER≈`.487`. Case values have coarsened digit accuracy; **ε at least .005** for the corner approximation; missing dispersion/source error is much larger and NOT included in ε. Do not claim verified hand-recurrence with per-k terms; the rounded cumulative values are an *approximate* tail read. This is NOT accepted as an official corner model.

### A7. Dependence, alternative models, safeguards

1. Both best research directions share full-time scoring intensity: first-half O0.5 **does not imply** total O2.5. In a case with independently factored FH and SH goals, **joint failure** `P(FH=0 AND FT<=2)=e^−λ_FT[1+λ_2H+(λ_2H²)/2]`. LOW λFT2.7/λSH1.4 gives approx .227, BASE3.1/1.5 .163, HIGH3.6/1.8 .121; weighted `.25×.227+.5×.163+.25×.121≈.169`. This is a *declared phase model* joint, not observed model calibration. Tail coupling/leading states and lineup changes omitted. The two top selections fail together in approximately 16.9% of declared scenarios.
2. `SHADOW_R2_RET04`: next different quantity O2.5 =.600 if allowed, but full-game rank1 banned when bench missing applies only when rank1; no formal promotion. `SHADOW_R2_XCARD6`: not independently certified, exact joint available for first-half and full-goal dimensions only. `SHADOW_RANK_DELTA`: NONE (no admitted same-line baseline). `SHADOW_BASE_ONLY`: ranks 1H O=.798, FT O=.599, corner O=.516, corner U=.484 but both corner scenarios diagnostic.
3. Goals-corners joint **NOT_ESTIMATED**: no invented independence. If comparing rank1 FH O0.5 with corner O10.5, Fréchet joint-loss bounds for losing masses `.210,.487`: `max(0,.210+.487−1)=0` to `min(.210,.487)=.210`; these are not a point probability. Corners over and under same line cannot both be won (their loss sets are disjoint).
4. Fixture arguments pull in opposite directions: both clubs' recent defence poor, Racing away goals conceded16/4, but Osasuna home total only5 goals in 4 games. The high-λ center is explicitly assumption-dependent. New coach, 23-player squad and incompletely sourced starters increase uncertainty; true probabilities can materially differ from the three scenario values. Keep `ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT / KNOWN_AT_PARTIAL`.
5. Price-free expected value: exact bookmaker odds unavailable and prohibited as input, so no EV relative to an offered market. We supply only sporting probabilities.

No unverified local controls applied. |

### Official 2026 postseason endpoint — decisive contract distinction

KBO's 7 October 2026 official postseason release https://www.koreabaseball.com/MediaNews/Notice/View.aspx?bdSe=12183 confirms **maximum 15 innings** and **a draw if still level after the 15th**. A drawn semi-playoff game is replayed after the series' nominal final scheduled game; there is no default MLB automatic runner. Official rules also describe suspended games and no ordinary rain-called result. Hence an ML proposition with unspecified operator settlement **cannot be certified as a two-way no-draw market**. Scenarios below assume *if draw then void* **only for conditional W/(W+L)**; a three-way ML, draw-as-loss, listed-pitcher, minimum-innings or suspended-game rule is **UNKNOWN_DEFINITION**. The O/U 9.5 endpoint is provisionally full-game scored runs including permitted extras, with suspended/void terms unknown. No extra-inning mass was empirically fitted.

## A5. Distribution, odds-free price arithmetic, sensitivities, departures

**League anchor, exact competition:** Full 2026 KBO regular season final official team table. Team runs sum: 825+806+821+679+769+719+688+723+706+565 = **7301**. Across 10 × 144 / 2 = **720 games**, mean **10.140 runs/game** (regular-season mixed stadiums/tie/extras). This is an independently evidenced exact-league rate, **not** a fitted 2026 KBO postseason total distribution; NO KBO postseason total SD/park coefficient available. The retired source-derived `LEAGUE_PROFILES` values are NOT ADMITTED under LP-v4. No MLB run rate is transferred to KBO.

**Season+L20 shrinkage:** Declared analyst k=60 season-equivalent games in denominator for an L20 weight 20/(20+60)=0.25, season weight .75; k **analyst scenario assumption**, not KBO-fitted. KIA RF/G .75(5.340)+.25(5.050)=**5.268**; KIA RA/G .75(4.611)+.25(3.700)=**4.383**. LG RF/G .75(5.021)+.25(4.100)=**4.791**; LG RA/G .75(5.042)+.25(4.300)=**4.857**. Opponent-adjusted *raw* pair using arithmetic scoring/conceding average: KIA (5.268+4.857)/2=**5.063**, LG (4.791+4.383)/2=**4.587**; raw total **9.650**, raw KIA margin **+0.476**. This is a transparent run-rate heuristic, not a possession/ERA or independently fitted generalized-linear model.

**Mechanism departure ledger (all scenarios, not data-fitted coefficients):** Jamsil's outdoor venue and unknown current weather could depress/increase run carry; no verified numeric 2026 Jamsil park factor, so do not assert a source-backed −X runs. Carrasco ERA 4.05 vs LG season 4.81 supports better run prevention, while 7 KIA LH batters with .267/.185 split offsets some of it; Shirakawa 4.75 vs KIA team 4.27 supports LG opportunity but poor 2-game LG split is too noisy to treat literally. Pen usage and 15-inning tail remain width and scenario effects without named numeric fitted coefficients. Home last-at-bat provides LG structural protection (late walk-off), but no sourced 2026 KBO exact-park home advantage coefficient. **No separate last-five adjustment**, no "due"/momentum or extrapolation of series 2–0 as a 2-run skill edge.

**Predeclared primary 25/50/25 low/base/high sporting scenarios** (all **illustrative assumptions**, not an admitted fitted KBO joint score model):

| Case | Weight | E(total runs), including possible extras | E(KIA margin) | Interpreted circumstance |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Low | .25 | **8.8** | **−0.30** | Carrasco lasts longer, LG home defence, suppressed early scoring |
| Base | .50 | **9.5** | **+0.40** | Balanced SP/bullpen, KIA recent run prevention partly persistent |
| High | .25 | **10.2** | **+1.10** | KIA platoon pressure/bullpen contribution outweighs starter uncertainty; longer upper-run tail |

Combined centres: E(T)=.25×8.8+.50×9.5+.25×10.2=**9.50**; E(M_KIA)=.25×(−.3)+.5×.4+.25×1.1=**+0.400**. E(KIA)=(9.5+.4)/2=**4.95**; E(LG)=(9.5−.4)/2=**4.55**. **Width assumptions:** σ(total)=**5.0 runs** using PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §3 negative-binomial lookup approximation *as a shape only*, not KBO-calibrated; σ(margin)=**4.8 runs** using toolkit §1 normal Φ approximation *as a shape only*. Means in 0.25 steps linearly interpolated for totals. Team-run SDs and Cov(KIA,LG) **NOT_COMPUTED**: without a fitted joint environment/innings/last-bat score grid they cannot be honestly inferred from independent σ(T)/σ(M) guesses. Case variance contributes further uncertainty; no exact joint score distribution or simulation was executed. True skew, capped tie/extras and stop-when-home-wins can alter values.

**Total O/U 9.5 — reproducible hand calculation:** From toolkit SD=5.0 negative-binomial table at 9.5 line: P(O9.5 | μ=8.75)=.381, at 9=.401; μ=8.80 gives .381+(.05/.25)(.401−.381)=**.385**. At μ=9.50 P(O)=**.443**. At μ=10 P(O)=.487, μ=10.25 P(O)=.509; at μ=10.20 P(O)=.487+(.20/.25)(.509−.487)=**.5046**. Mixture O = .25×.385+.50×.443+.25×.5046 = **.4444**. Under = **1−.4444=.5556**. The line is half-run, so no total push in a normally completed scored game. `p_base(O)=.443`, `p_base(U)=.557`. Numerical table interpolation ε approximately .003 (a stated approximation, excludes model uncertainty); model uncertainty far exceeds ε.

**Decisive winner / ML arithmetic, conditional on no official 15-inning draw:** Signed KIA margin M normal approximation by scenario with σ=4.8 and means −0.30, +0.40, +1.10. Toolkit Φ: z=−.0625, .0833, .2292 → Φ(z) ≈ **.475, .533, .591**. Mixture P(KIA | game decisive) = .25×.475+.50×.533+.25×.591 = **.5330**; LG =1−.5330=**.4670**. `p_base=.533/.467`; ε≈.003. These are *conditional* price approximations that ignore the detailed last-bat/15-inning tie kernel; **P(draw in 15 innings)=NOT_ESTIMATED**. Unconditional W/P/L/V cannot be filled faithfully without P(draw) and the unspecified operator action. **Contract-definition gate open**; no false assert that P(KIA win)=.533 of all fixtures. Sensitivities: KIA decisive p .475–.591, LG .409–.525; Under U .615–.495, Over .385–.505 in low-to-high cases. Total centre vs line: line 9.5−μ 0 runs; signed margin z base .0833. Core 2026 League mean 10.14 is higher than scenario mean 9.50 by .64; fully attributed to recent lower-scoring L20/shrinkage, exact starter/venue differences and *assumed* postseason state, **not** a source-calibrated venue coefficient.

**Baseline and ranking controls:** `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` exact 2026 KBO, postseason semi-playoff, full game incl 15-inning tie/possible extras, same moneyline action and total line: **NOT_ESTIMATED** because an independently assembled complete same-era postseason distribution, tie convention and variance/total frequencies are absent. Observed league-only mean10.14 is *not* enough to fabricate p_baseleague. `TEAM_BASELINE_P`: NOT_COVERED for KBO. Original rank1 gate: p=.5556 < .62 and gap vs best non-complementary KIA conditional=.0226 < .04 → **FAIL / unqualified**; Rank2 floor p=.533 < .58 → FAIL. Gate retains conditional-v-unconditional comparability caveat; it cannot be formally scored without ML operator action. `SHADOW_GATE_RET03`: KIA ML=.533 non-complementary; same failure. `SHADOW_R2_RET04`: NONE at p≥.58; `SHADOW_R2_XCARD6`: NONE; `SHADOW_RANK_DELTA`: NONE (baseline missing); `SHADOW_BASE_ONLY`: same four approximate ranks. **Dependent risk:** Under and KIA ML share game states; no fitted joint score grid, so exact joint failure unavailable. If loss probabilities l_U=.4444 and l_KIA=.4670 *conditional on decisive*, provisional Frechet bound [max(0,.4444+.467−1), min(.4444,.467)] = **[0,.4444]** on a common decisive conditional space; this is not an exact unconditional joint because of unresolved tie mass. Never multiply these losses or claim mechanically protected.

**Contested assumptions requiring caution:** Scenario σ(total)=5.0 and σ(margin)=4.8, k=60 and low/base/high centres are analyst inputs, not statistically estimated. Small shifts of ±1 run move Under9.5 across ~50%; momentum/historic H2H cannot justify >62% forecast. No verified 2026 Jamsil park-specific total SD or current wind. Shut-off/suspended-game action and true postseason inning kernel may shift totals; not priced exactly.

## A6. Venue, environment, strategy and rules

- **Exact venue:** Jamsil, LG bats last, and cannot need the bottom ninth if ahead after KIA's ninth; walk-off and extra-inning score distributions are not the same as a neutral park. Home advantage is a mechanism, *not a calibrated numerical estimate*.
- **Series state:** KIA 2–0 best of five. LG must win to continue. KIA manager said he would consider quick removal of Shirakawa if innings 1–2 show problems and indicated possible relief appearances by Lee Eui-ri, Jeong Hae-yeong, Kim Tae-hyeong. The LG plan for Carrasco followed by relief arm(s) is reported but exact available bench and pitch limits incomplete. These alter tail/transition risks; exact branch masses UNKNOWN.
- **Rules:** postseason innings 1–9 standard; innings 10–15 max; tied after 15 recorded draw and played again later in series; no regulation-only assumption. Official series requires an eventual winner, but **Game 3** can itself end tied. 2026 KBO postseason disallows normal no-game/rain-called dispositions; suspended-game rule applies. The user's moneyline action on suspended/drawn match remains `UNKNOWN_DEFINITION`.
- **Weather:** no venue-specific 18:30 KST temperature/wind/rain forecast with source timestamp was reliably obtained; `WEATHER_NOT_RETRIEVED`, no directional scoring adjustment claimed.
- **Prohibited signals:** no betting odds, tips, market-implied p, market lines other than owner-supplied contracts, live event data or outcome exposure used. 20 October target game not inspected for scoring after request.

#### A4. Distribution, comparator, expected-score arithmetic and sensitivity

**Competition anchor:** Current 2026/27 J1 through Matchweek 9 = 262 goals / 90 matches = 2.911 per match = 1.456 per team-match. No verified exact J1 home-away goal split; use explicit *judgmental* +8% home / −8% away multiplicative effects, not a measured league effect. Do not confuse these assumed multipliers with learned weights. Input season sample n9 per club; hand shrink count k=10 team-match equivalents toward 1.456, so effective team weight 9/19 and league weight 10/19.

- Machida shrunk GF = (22 + 10×1.456)/19 = 1.924; GA = (10+14.56)/19 = 1.293.
- Fukuoka shrunk GF = (11+14.56)/19 = 1.345; GA = (13+14.56)/19 = 1.451.
- Unadjusted neutral expected Machida = 1.924×1.451/1.456 = **1.918**; Fukuoka = 1.345×1.293/1.456 = **1.195**.
- Assumed venue effect: Machida 1.918×1.08 = **2.071**; Fukuoka 1.195×0.92 = **1.099**.
- Analyst adjustment for Machida 15 Oct Singapore travel/two-rest-day turnaround and possible rotation: **−0.22 Machida goals** (not empirically measured); Fukuoka Kamijima defensive suspension: **+0.05 Machida goals** (judgmental); combined 2.071−0.22+0.05 ≈ **1.90**. For Fukuoka, cautious away-strength/selection offset **−0.05 goals** on 1.099 → **1.05**, explicitly a discretionary uncertainty adjustment, not proof of a causal decrement from the absent Shimoda. These effects should not be treated as independent precise facts.
- **Base distribution:** regulation H~Poisson(1.90), A~Poisson(1.05), independent conditional on analyst scenario; μ_total=**2.95**, margin=**+0.85**. Poisson total SD √2.95≈1.72; corners Poisson √8.4≈2.90 before possible overdispersion. Low-score draw dependence remains unmeasured.
- **First-half separate state:** 2026/27 home-team and away-team overall combined 1H historical observations 1.333 and 1.111; their venue-specific 1.0/1.0 n4/n3 are not robust. Mix pre-declared roughly 70% season team average (~1.222) with 30% venue average (1.0) = 1.155, then discount ~0.055 for potential slow/rotated early match tempo => **μ_1H=1.10**. This is an analyst assumption, not a measured separate phase fit. Fukuoka historic slower 1H is a downside sensitivity; separate phase means the first-half and second-half rates sum to each total-goal scenario (see below).
- **Corners independent rate input:** Machida own 40/9=4.44, against 36/9=4.00; Fukuoka own 40/9=4.44, against 29/9=3.22. Expected home corner centre (4.44+3.22)/2=3.83; away (4.44+4.00)/2=4.22; raw μ=8.05. Shrink the average combined fixture mean of 8.06 from 18 team-match observations toward league 9.1 using a judgmental pseudo-weight 9 league matches: (18×8.06+9×9.1)/27≈**8.41** ⇒ base **μ_corners=8.40**. Team allocation ~4.0 / 4.4 is approximation; no score-state-dependent covariance has been independently fitted. Provider measurement and overdispersion are material limits.

**Three coherent scenarios (prior to pricing):**

| Case | Weight | Machida λ | Fukuoka λ | Total λ | 1H total λ | 2H total λ | Corner λ | Dominant risk |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| LOW Machida / adverse | 0.25 | 1.65 | 1.20 | 2.85 | 1.00 | 1.85 | 7.40 | Machida tired, Fukuoka transitions |
| BASE | 0.50 | 1.90 | 1.05 | 2.95 | 1.10 | 1.85 | 8.40 | Expected selection/tempo |
| HIGH Machida / favourable | 0.25 | 2.15 | 0.90 | 3.05 | 1.20 | 1.85 | 9.40 | More Machida pressure, more corners |

Within each case model goal counts as independent Poissons; total full goals is first- plus second-half split with scenario-consistent positive phase means (each team's split assigned in proportion to its full λ). Conditional corner counts are a distinct Poisson; a *shared scenario* induces directional dependence but not fully captures score-state correlations. Case widths are subjective stress tests, NOT statistical confidence intervals.

**Probability arithmetic, using published toolkit table, interpolation and Poisson recurrence:**

1. Machida scores ≥1: `1 − e^(−λ_H)` = (0.808, 0.850, 0.884) across low/base/high. Mixture `0.25×0.808 + 0.50×0.850 + 0.25×0.884 = 0.848` ⇒ **84.8%**; non-scoring/failure = **15.2%**. The 0–0 and 0–x finishing-failure branch is substantial and rises with major rotation; XI unconfirmed.
2. Regulation home/draw/away from 2D Poisson score table interpolating λH and λA: LOW ≈ (0.479/0.243/0.278); BASE ≈ (0.572/0.222/0.206); HIGH ≈ (0.660/0.194/0.146). Weighted ≈ (**0.571/0.220/0.209**). Thus Machida-or-draw `1−P(away win)=0.791` ⇒ **79.1%**. The away upset mass ≈**20.9%**, not zero. Half-step/table interpolation creates ~0.5 percentage point rounding/approximation tolerance.
3. Corners Under10.5 for means (7.4,8.4,9.4): Poisson `Σ(k=0..10) e^(−μ) μ^k / k!` ≈ (0.8705,0.7743,0.6568). Mixture `0.25×0.8705+0.5×0.7743+0.25×0.6568=0.7690` ⇒ **76.9%**, opposite Over **23.1%**. A real high-variance/negative-binomial corner process could lower this probability; no calibrated dispersion data available.
4. First-half Over0.5 for μ (1.0,1.1,1.2): `1−e^(−μ)` = (0.6321,0.6671,0.6988), mixture = **0.6663** ⇒ **66.6%**, Under0.5 **33.4%**. Fukuoka's long-run conservative first halves prevent a stronger assertion.
5. Full total Over2.5 for μ (2.85,2.95,3.05): `1−e^(−μ)(1+μ+μ²/2)` ≈ (0.543,0.566,0.588); mixture ≈ **56.5%**, Under **43.5%**.
6. Under3.5 for μ (2.85,2.95,3.05): `e^(−μ)(1+μ+μ²/2+μ³/6)` ≈ (0.681,0.659,0.636); mixture ≈ **65.9%**.

**Coherence checks:** For each supplied half-point line, W+L=1 and P=V=0 under normal-play convention; P(Over0.5 1H)+P(Under0.5 1H)=1; P(Over2.5)+P(Under2.5)=1; corner complements sum1. Regulation 1X=home+draw (0.571+0.220≈0.791). First-half total rates are nonnegative and less than full totals per scenario. All p_card are UNCALI­BRATED analyst estimates; number of apparent decimals does not confer precision.

**Comparator:** `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1` exact J1 current-season Poisson total anchor μ=2.911 yields Over2.5 roughly 0.56, Under3.5 roughly 0.67; corner μ=9.1 gives Under10.5 roughly 0.69. Separate J1 historical 1H 1.07 mean yields Over0.5 ≈0.657. These are **diagnostic provisional comparators**, not proven same-provider frozen baselines. Exact preissue J1 1X base-rate rows/home edge and current-season 1H league rates were not independently captured, so baseline coverage is not certified; no incremental predictive claim.

**Sensitivity:** If first-half mean decreases to 0.90 (conservative Fukuoka tempo), Over0.5 falls to about 59.3%; at μ1.30 it rises to about 72.7%. At corner μ9.4 Under10.5 falls to ~65.7% versus ~87.1% at μ7.4; extra overdispersion not quantified. Finishing regression and unverified Machida rotation can reduce the 84.8% score-once estimate. Changes in match-day XI should require repricing before reissue; any post-start change is a new timestamped addendum, never backdated.

#### A7. Contract, source and dependency integrity

- Matchwinner and 1X are 90-minute outcomes with separate draw mass; no extra-time or penalty states for this ordinary league match.
- Corners depend on provider definition and score-state exposure. J.League official CK plus independently checkable ESPN `wonCorners` is the intended research/settlement route; the latter was not verified as available for THIS fixture, hence corners remain INFORMATIONAL despite numeric estimate.
- Missing exact-game XI, bench and set-piece roles prevent an official supported player market and restrict numeric confidence in team total/double chance. No line is calibrated and no model-shadow information is used.
- **Joint failure:** team goal O0.5 failure and 1X failure are nested only in some score states, not independent: scoreless Machida with Fukuoka scoring causes both to fail, but a 0–0 fails Machida-to-score while *passes* 1X. Under10.5 CK failure co-varies with chasing and pressure. A validated joint covariance model is absent: `JOINT_FAILURE_NOT_CERTIFIED`; do not multiply marginal losses as if independent.
- **Gate:** Numeric first rank 84.8%, second 79.1%, gap 5.7 points, epsilon ~0.5 point; numerical threshold satisfied, operational qualification `RANK1_UNSTABLE_LINEUP_AND_EVIDENCE`. Rank2 numeric floor passes, but not certified. Sensitivity of 1H goal probability spans ~59%–73%; it is not a 90% pick.
- Original owner-supplied lines preserved and both sides assessed. Any monetary or price-based expected value is NOT COMPUTABLE within SPORTS_ONLY/MARKET_BLIND research, as no price/odds has been supplied or read.

**Conditions:** damp/fresh morning wicket, cloud/drizzle, Australia bowl first, four pace specialists; no independently captured exact strip moisture/grass reading or authoritative hourly radar. Weather risk higher for match draw than for a *completed* Test first-innings line, but delayed/reduced playable exposure creates unknown void/completion probability. Australian fielding unit has good recent first-Test evidence, but SA made batting changes; do not count changes twice.

**Venue samples conflict:** long-run St George's Park Test batting-first mean about 313 in one ground catalogue, while three recent completed first-innings records are much higher; the latter is very small and selected for matches reaching scorecard completion. Without matched surface, weather, opponent and innings-order distributions they are **context only**, not exact-line `BASELINE_P`. Historical large scoring at this ground included former keeper Kyle Verreynne, absent today.

#### A4. One declared hand-derived marginal distribution and sensitivity

**League anchor and team adjustment:** exact-series league-only mean/SD not published in reviewed active profile (`LEAGUE_PROFILES.md`; `BASE_RATES_REGISTER.md` cricket=NOT_YET_DERIVED). Do not mislabel an arbitrary mean as a fitted league baseline. Hand scenario's base 270 is between SA's six-opponent-filtered 290.5 and Australia's recent three first-innings conceded mean 258.0 (midpoint 274.25), lowered approximately four runs qualitatively for damp new-ball conditions. This weighting is **analyst-chosen**, not competition-validated; SA and Australia match structures differ. Alternatives 236 and 312 intentionally reflect collapse / enduring-innings uncertainty. Widths 105–125 are deliberately wide **unfitted** dispersion assumptions, not historical SDs. Five-over base 12, low 10, high 17 reflect one 15/1 prior observation, damp seam expectation, debutant/top-order volatility and boundary-driven tails; phase widths 7–9 are similarly uncalibrated. No after-the-fact fit or code execution.

**Scenarios are frozen before computing probabilities:** `LOW=0.25; BASE=0.50; HIGH=0.25`. For each nonnegative integer total `X` use a truncated continuous-Normal continuity approximation:

`P(X≤k) ≈ [Φ((k+0.5−μ)/σ) − Φ((−0.5−μ)/σ)] / [1−Φ((−0.5−μ)/σ)]`.

This avoids negative-run support in the normal approximation, but **does not** model wicket-dependent innings stops, declarations, phase covariance or discrete over transitions. It is only an analyst numerical illustration.

| Component | Regime | μ runs | σ runs | Normal z at half-line | Approx. P(Under), truncated | Source/assumption |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 1st innings | LOW | 236 | 105 | +0.443 | 66.7% | Damp/collapse and scarce top-order scoring, subjective |
| 1st innings | BASE | 270 | 115 | +0.109 | 53.9% | 274 midpoint proxy − ~4 damp condition adjustment |
| 1st innings | HIGH | 312 | 125 | −0.236 | 40.3% | Batting recovery on flat, older ball or long innings |
| First 5 overs | LOW | 10 | 7 | +0.500 | 66.9% | Tight four-seamer new-ball conditions |
| First 5 overs | BASE | 12 | 8 | +0.188 | 54.8% | One prior 15/1 score, adjusted for damp conditions |
| First 5 overs | HIGH | 17 | 9 | −0.389 | 33.1% | Aiden Markram/other opener finds boundaries early |

**Weighted arithmetic:**

- `P(SA I1 Under282.5)=0.25×0.667 + 0.50×0.539 + 0.25×0.403 = 0.5370` → **53.7%**; Over = `1−0.5370=46.3%`.
- `P(SA five-over Under13.5)≈0.25×0.669 + 0.50×0.548 + 0.25×0.331=0.5240` → **52.4%**; Over ≈ **47.6%**. Display rounded to one decimal only as illustrative values; intermediate approximations introduce small arithmetic error, and model uncertainty is **much larger**.
- Approximate untruncated weighted expected innings mean = `0.25×236 + 0.50×270 + 0.25×312 = 272` runs; five-over mean `0.25×10+0.50×12+0.25×17=12.75` runs. Nonnegative truncation slightly changes those expectations. No mean-wickets figure assumed.
- True model-assumption sensitivity is materially wider than the table rounding: changing innings expected mean by ±35 runs against σ around115 shifts Under282.5 by approximately **12 percentage points**; changing five-over mean by ±3 with width 8 changes its Under13.5 probability by roughly **14 points**. These are **scenario stress tests, not confidence intervals or evidence of skill**.

**Test structure omitted by this approximation:** a fall of wicket can slow or accelerate scoring, 80-over new ball, rain and light control available time, and declarations/all-out terminate innings. Actual innings variance need not be Normal. No independent conditional validation, no opponent-adjusted ball-by-ball Test model, no exact joint CDF or simulation: such claims would be false. The expected 272 is an **illustrative distribution mean**, not a reliable predicted final innings score. `P(draw)` is not estimated.

All unconditional W/P/L/V values including probability of incomplete/no-play endpoints are `NOT_COMPUTED`; do not silently call these conditional event distributions operator-certifiable outcomes. Exactly four original contract alternatives assessed; no four distinct diversified targets supported.

#### A7. Contract dependence, counterfactuals and match winner

Within the same endpoint, `P(Under)+P(Over)=1.000`. Across the two endpoints, first-five runs are a subset of the eventual innings total, so outcomes are **not independent**. A very fast first 30 balls can still precede a total below 283 after a collapse; a slow first 30 balls can still precede 300+ later. No exact covariance or copula has been sourced, so joint probability remains within the honest Fréchet bounds stated above. Do not claim pairing reduces loss to zero or has any demonstrated advantage. `RANK1_UNSTABLE` and `BELOW_FLOOR` remain disclosed.

Australia is the **qualitative potential match winner**, not a statistical 60–70% call. The four-seam attack won the toss; South Africa's revamped batting remains exposed, and Australia are 1–0 ahead. But Gqeberha home familiarity, Rabada and especially variable playable time can cause South Africa to win or a draw. No verified Test-match result-state model or weather/overs survival distribution exists in this run; therefore `P(AUS WIN)=NOT_ESTIMATED`, `P(DRAW)=NOT_ESTIMATED`, `P(SA WIN)=NOT_ESTIMATED`, and those must not be set to values summing to one by invention.

### Decision block

Napoli have won all six current-season LBA/EuroCup games, but the small three-game Italian-league sample and a significantly easier European schedule do not justify a large advantage against defending champion Milano. Milano's three foreign-player LBA exclusions remain unconfirmed, as do target-match starters. The league anchor of approximately 172 total points supports a **very slight Under 174.5** preference; Napoli's home/momentum lean is weaker than the forecast errors. **Neither proposed PICK meets the selection gates.**

| Rank | Role | Tag | Exact supplied proposition | p_card | p_base | BASELINE_P | Distance from base mean / z | Method | Main failure route |
|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | PICK (WEAK / UNGATED) | SUPPLIED | Combined total **Under 174.5** points, including OT | **54.2%** | 54.7% | 55.7% | +2.5 points / +0.119 | Three-state final-total normal | Napoli shooting continues at current 40%+ three-point rate; close game OT |
| 2 | PICK (WEAK / BELOW FLOOR) | SUPPLIED | **Napoli −1.5** final-game points, including OT | **51.5%** | 51.5% | 52.1% | Napoli margin line +1.5 vs m=+2.1 / z=+0.0375 for cover | Three-state final-margin normal | Milano depth, perimeter shot creation, late-game finishing |
| 3 | INFORMATIONAL / COMPLEMENT | SUPPLIED | **Milano +1.5** final-game points, including OT | **48.5%** | 48.5% | 47.9% | Same margin as rank 2, opposite side | Exact complement of rank 2 | Napoli home offence and 3–0 league form |
| 4 | INFORMATIONAL / COMPLEMENT | SUPPLIED | Combined total **Over 174.5** points, including OT | **45.8%** | 45.3% | 44.3% | Same total line as rank 1 | Exact complement of rank 1 | Both teams held closer to LBA mean pace/efficiency |

**Probability precision:** two-decimal-place hand interpolation in the PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT normal Φ table; numerical lookup ε≈0.002 per row, excluding **much larger** real parameter/model uncertainty. Primary rank gap between first two ~0.027, not a precision tie. **Baseline coverage:** 4/4 approximate league normal rows; 0/4 prospectively calibrated empirical line-CDFs.

**Rank-1 gate:** `RANK1_UNSTABLE`: p1=.542 (<.620); best noncomplementary eligible original candidate Napoli −1.5=.515; gap .027 (<.040). **Rank-2 floor:** `BELOW_FLOOR`, .515 < .580. No unsupported claim of strong/qualified picks. **Joint failure** (Under loses when T≥175, Napoli −1.5 loses when M≤1): unconditional losing masses .458 and .485; Fréchet bounds `[max(0,.458+.485−1), min(.458,.485)] = [0,.458]`, because residual T–M dependence unmeasured; exact joint NOT_COMPUTED. **Winner:** Napoli about **55.1%**, Milano 44.9%, with material uncertainty; win status has no draw at full completion.

**Shadows:** RET03 alternate Napoli −1.5 at .515 -> fails both floor/gap; RET04 different quantity Napoli −1.5 below .58 -> NONE; XCARD6 NONE at ≥.58; RANK_DELTA NONE (no ≥.58 primary with baseline); BASE_ONLY order Under .547, Napoli −1.5 .515, Milano +1.5 .485, Over .453; gate still fails. No model shadow run. **Adjustment:** the Napoli side is sensitive to the unfitted −2-point margin quality-prior and unavailable foreign-player roster; `ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT` sensitivity diagnostic, not a proved personnel effect. Supplied rows all assessed; none omitted.

#### A4. Coherent final-score approximation, baseline and hand calculations

**Exact 2026/27 league sample:** 24 regulation-format LBA matches in completed R1–R3 shown in `https://www.repubblica.it/sport/dirette/basket/serie-a-2026/calendario/`, independently spot-checked via LBA, Basketball-Reference teams and Eurosport. Their 24 completed full-score totals by round:

- R1: `203,158,161,162,194,169,159,150` (sum **1,356**, n=8).
- R2: `183,192,187,178,169,162,147,169` (sum **1,387**, n=8).
- R3: `169,140,183,179,191,171,179,179` (sum **1,391**, n=8).

Combined `1,356+1,387+1,391=4,134` points /24 => **T_league =172.25**. Sum of squared deviations from 172.25 is **5,710.50**, so sample total SD `sqrt(5710.50/23)=15.76`. The corresponding R1–R3 home-minus-away margins in chronological order:

- R1 `+15,+12,-5,+4,+8,-19,-21,+24` (sum +18)
- R2 `-3,+6,+9,+38,-5,-8,+3,-1` (sum +39)
- R3 `+1,-8,+29,-7,+1,-7,+7,-19` (sum −3).

Total home margin `(+18+39−3)/24=+2.25`; squared-deviation sum 4,814.50 so sample margin SD `sqrt(4814.50/23)=14.47`. These are tiny in-season benchmarks, **not fitted predictive residuals or a robust 2026/27 calibration**, and OT source tags not audited match-by-match. The normal league baseline at the exact original lines is:

- `p_BASELINE(Under174.5)≈Φ((174.5−172.25)/15.76)=Φ(0.143)=0.557` and Over `.443`.
- `p_BASELINE(Napoli−1.5)≈Φ((2.25−1.5)/14.47)=Φ(0.052)=0.521` and Milano +1.5 `.479`. Generic league home team, NOT Napoli-specific.

**Shrinkage point-estimate construction:** exact 2026/27 league mean per team `172.25/2=86.125`. To avoid raw three-game overfitting, admit only `w=.40` of each current LBA team-for and opposite team-against departure (analyst-assumed shrinkage; no cross-validated coefficient):

- `Napoli offence departure =97.333−86.125=+11.208`; Milano defence/allowed departure `77−86.125=−9.125`; pre-home Napoli centre `86.125+.40(11.208−9.125)=86.958`.
- `Milano offence departure =87−86.125=+0.875`; Napoli allowed departure `82.667−86.125=−3.458`; pre-home Milano centre `86.125+.40(.875−3.458)=85.092`.
- League observed home edge +2.25 applied symmetrically to the expected margin: `Napoli 86.958+1.125=88.083`; `Milano 85.092−1.125=83.967`; total `172.050`, unadjusted margin `+4.116`.
- **Quality/rotation uncertainty:** Milano defending LBA champion with deeper EuroLeague opposition and three unconfirmed domestic exclusions. A subjective *quality prior* shifts **1.0 point to Milano and −1.0 point from Napoli**, preserving combined total while reducing base Napoli margin to about `+2.1`: base Napoli **87.1**, Milano **85.0**, total **172.1**. The numeric prior is NOT derived from a measured coefficient; its impact is exposed and varied rather than passed as verified team strength. Napoli plays 14 Oct and Milano 15 Oct, but no arbitrary extra numeric fatigue penalty is layered on because both faced continental travel/rest burdens.

**Three state final-score scenarios, fixed before evaluation (uncertainties: home adjustment, roster choices, tempo, early shooting efficiency):**

| State | Weight | E[T] pts | E[M]=Napoli−Milano pts | Conditional E[Napoli] | Conditional E[Milano] | SD(T) | SD(M) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| LOW Napoli / lower tempo | .25 | 166.0 | −3.0 | 81.5 | 84.5 | 21 | 16 |
| BASE | .50 | 172.0 | +2.1 | 87.05 | 84.95 | 21 | 16 |
| HIGH Napoli / higher tempo | .25 | 179.0 | +7.2 | 93.1 | 85.9 | 21 | 16 |

Both SD widths are **subjective widened** from observed league raw SD of 15.76 and 14.47; this is not a claim of fitted uncertainty. Mixture E[T]=`.25×166+.50×172+.25×179=172.25`; E[M]=`.25×(−3)+.50×2.1+.25×7.2=+2.10`; implied mean scores `87.175` and `85.075`. A total/positive-margin covariance and overtime-event model are not separately estimated; marginal prices are coherent complements but exact joint losses are **not** inferable.

**Formula:** `Φ(z)` is PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §1 normal cumulative table (two-decimal interpolation). Each state: `P(Under174.5 | s)=Φ((174.5−μ_T,s)/21)`; `P(Napoli−1.5 | s)=Φ((μ_M,s−1.5)/16)`; mixture `p=.25p_low+.50p_base+.25p_high`.

| Outcome | LOW: z → p | BASE: z → p | HIGH: z → p | Mixture arithmetic |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Under 174.5 | `8.5/21=.405 → .657` | `2.5/21=.119 → .547` | `−4.5/21=−.214 → .415` | `.25(.657)+.50(.547)+.25(.415)=.542` |
| Napoli −1.5 | `(−3−1.5)/16=−.281 → .389` | `(2.1−1.5)/16=.0375 → .515` | `(7.2−1.5)/16=.356 → .639` | `.25(.389)+.50(.515)+.25(.639)=.515` |
| Milano +1.5 | exact `1−p(Napoli−1.5)` | — | — | `1−.515=.485` |
| Over 174.5 | exact `1−p(Under174.5)` | — | — | `1−.542=.458` |
| Napoli eventual winner | `Φ(−3/16)=.426` | `Φ(2.1/16)=.552` | `Φ(7.2/16)=.674` | `.25(.426)+.50(.552)+.25(.674)=.551` |

All half-point lines have no push for a played completed game; `P(win)+P(loss)=1`; no bookmaker odds used. **Overtime limitation:** the moments are for *final* outcomes and implicitly absorb historical OT in source game scores; no separately proven OT occurrence rate, tie-at-regulation mass, or added-five-min points distribution was fitted. | 1 | PICK — BELOW GATE | SUPPLIED | Own batting innings **Under 110.5** runs (full 20-over/all-out endpoint) | **0.560** | 0.568 | BASELINE_UNAVAILABLE | 110.5 − 107.0=+3.5 runs; ≈+0.12 mixed SD | Linked normal scenario CDF | Zimbabwe survives new ball and goes 120–145; India bowls first on flat strip |
| 2 | PICK — BELOW FLOOR | SUPPLIED | Own innings **Over 33.5** runs after 6 completed overs | **0.527** | 0.531 | BASELINE_UNAVAILABLE | 33.5 − 34.75=−1.25 runs; ≈−0.09 mixed SD | Same phase/remainder mixture | India takes two early wickets or Zimbabwe starts cautiously |
| 3 | INFORMATIONAL | SUPPLIED | Own innings **Under 33.5** runs after 6 overs | **0.473** | 0.469 | BASELINE_UNAVAILABLE | as previous | Exact mathematical complement within completed phase | Opening pair hits one extra boundary |
| 4 | INFORMATIONAL | SUPPLIED | Own batting innings **Over 110.5** runs | **0.440** | 0.432 | BASELINE_UNAVAILABLE | as first | Exact mathematical complement within full-innings assumption | Middle order collapse, all-out early |

**Baseline coverage:** 0/4 same-line league-only numerical baselines; two separate endpoints lack an eligible population cohort.
**Rank-1 gate:** `RANK1_UNSTABLE`, p1≈.560 <.620 minimum, best noncomplementary alternative `6-over Over 33.5` p≈.527, gap≈.033 <.040; Φ approximation error ε≈.003 due rounded z and scenario interpolation, not a borderline precision gate.
**Rank-2 floor:** `BELOW_FLOOR` (0.527 < 0.580).
**Joint failure:** loss of Rank1 ≈0.440; loss of Rank2 ≈0.473; from shared scenarios but no performed bivariate CDF integration, Fréchet [max(0,.440+.473−1), min(.440,.473)]=[0, **0.440**], NOT_INDEPENDENT, not a precise joint rate. Both decisive on normal uninterrupted innings branch only; overall void probability UNKNOWN.
**Shadow gate / Rank 2:** RET03 best noncomplementary Over33.5=.527, fails; RET04 alternative different quantity with p>=.58 NONE; XCARD6 qualifying p>=.58 NONE; `SHADOW_RANK_DELTA` NONE because BASELINE_P unavailable; `SHADOW_BASE_ONLY` Under110.5 (.568), Over33.5 (.531), Under33.5 (.469), Over110.5 (.432), no gate PASS. All shadows NOT_PRIMARY.
**Adjustment dependence:** uncertainty branches alter values; removing strength/opponent/uncertainty adjustments can switch a near-50 powerplay lean; do not advertise dominance. No adjustment of ≥.25 of the total SD proven from source data, all scenario parameters explicitly ANALYST assumptions.
**Potential match winner:** **India Women**, qualitative preference due opponent-strength gulf and first-match win; **India / Zimbabwe / tie probabilities NOT_ESTIMATED** because no supported complete two-team chase, rain and Super Over distribution. This winner is not a fifth numeric supplied contract.
**Supplied rows not selected:** all four fully displayed; bottom two are mutually complementary alternatives, not additional independent high-confidence bets.

#### A4. Coherent hand-built joint distribution proxy, arithmetic and scenario sensitivity

**Common event-state simplifying construction:** Zimbabwe powerplay X (runs in first 36 legal balls) and remaining innings R (next possible 84 legal balls, or terminal early) sum Y=X+R. Approximate X and R with independent normals **conditional on a shared low/base/high team innings environment**. Therefore phase/total are positively correlated from shared X and shared scenario. This is not a legal-ball transition engine: no explicit wicket hazard, early all-out stop, chase censor, DLS, dismissal dependence or target distribution. If innings does not reach 6.0, the proxy's powerplay checkpoint is not itself an operator-observable event. Use only as visibly conditional preliminary analyst distribution.

| Joint scoring scenario | Weight | μ_X first 6 | σ_X | μ_R remainder | σ_R | Implied μ_Y = μ_X+μ_R | Implied σ_Y |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Low, early collapse/cautious | .25 | 29.0 | 12.0 | 61.0 | sqrt(24²−12²)=20.78 | 90.0 | 24.0 |
| Base, below-par moderate | .50 | 34.5 | 13.0 | 71.5 | sqrt(27²−13²)=23.66 | 106.0 | 27.0 |
| High, settled top order | .25 | 41.0 | 15.0 | 85.0 | sqrt(30²−15²)=25.98 | 126.0 | 30.0 |

**Input basis:** L20 Zimbabwe 110.15; shorter-window L5 122; India conceded L20 121.35 but against stronger sides and L5 only 86.4 chiefly weaker opponents; first game Zimbabwe 114 and six over 34; corroborated other powerplays 27–41. Analyst centres 90/106/126 and widths 24/27/30 are not sample estimators: choices bracket matchup/selection/toss regime, not fitted coefficients. All source matches predate scheduled start.

**Mixture centre and width, by hand:** E[X]=.25(29)+.5(34.5)+.25(41)=**34.75**. E[Y]=.25(90)+.5(106)+.25(126)=**107.00**. Var X = E(σ_X²)+Var(μ_X)=176.75+18.0625=194.8125; SD X≈13.96. Var Y= (.25·576+.5·729+.25·900)+(72.25+.5+90.25)=733.5+163=896.5; SD Y≈29.94. Cov(X,Y)=E Var(X|scenario)+Cov(μX,μY)=176.75+54.25=**231.0** runs²; implied correlation 231/(13.96·29.94)≈**0.55**. Positive association, **not independence across contract quantities**.

**At 110.5, use toolkit normal CDF:** z=(110.5−μ_Y)/σ_Y per scenario. Low z=20.5/24≈+0.85, Φ≈.8023; Base z=4.5/27≈+.17, Φ≈.5675; High z=−15.5/30≈−.52, Φ≈.3015. P(Under110.5)=.25·.8023+.50·.5675+.25·.3015=.200575+.283750+.075375=**.559700**, approximately **56.0%**; complementary Over=.440300 ≈44.0%. Base-only Under=.5675, Over=.4325.

**At 33.5, use toolkit normal CDF:** z=(33.5−μ_X)/σ_X. Low z=4.5/12=+.375, Φ≈.6462; Base z=−1/13≈−.077, Φ≈.4693; High z=−7.5/15=−.50, Φ≈.3085. P(Under33.5)=.25·.6462+.50·.4693+.25·.3085=.161550+.234650+.077125=**.473325**, approx **47.3%**; complementary Over=.526675 ≈52.7%. Base-only Under=.4693, Over=.5307.

**State constraints and censoring:** X and R theoretically can be negative in a continuous normal proxy (worst phase scenario Φ(−29/12)≈0.008, low remainder Φ(−61/20.78)≈.002). No discrete exact enumeration or strict nonnegative truncation was applied, so printed Φ is an approximation and should not be described as an exact cricket outcome probability. Wickets and exposure affect tails, especially full 20-over all-out. If Zimbabwe chase a small India total, reaching target terminates the innings; winning delivery can overshoot target (PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §7), and this proxy does not implement that exact censor. Exact bat-first/chase weights uncertain because toss unverified; 50:50 prior cannot replace toss evidence and no robust bat/chase parameter pair exists. The estimates primarily represent batting-first/unconstrained-full-innings analogue and are CONDITIONAL, not actual operator probabilities.

**Practical sensitivity (illustrative changes in source-uncertain centre, not claimed confidence intervals):**

| Scenario question | Direction for Under110.5 | Direction for Over33.5 | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Zimbabwe loses early wicket / superior India swing | Higher | Lower | top-order disruption and dot-ball pressure |
| New ball on same Raipur surface produces initial partnership | Lower | Higher | balls 1–36 early boundaries |
| Zimbabwe bat second chasing India target ≫110 | near prior proxy | could be higher | need chase rate and overshoot model |
| Zimbabwe bat second with target ≤110 | Under more likely, unless final over six/extra overshoots | cannot infer | censor/target state must be computed |
| Toss, unconfirmed XI and current pitch remain missing | widen uncertainty | widen uncertainty | no extra numeric certainty from prior squad |
| Rain-shortened below 20 overs / powerplay changes | operator action may VOID | action may VOID | DLS target is not 20.0 innings checkpoint |

**Unadjusted comparison:** published first match 114 (only +3.5 above full-innings line), 6.0 34/1 (only +.5 above powerplay line). An unadjusted simple form-anchor with μY=110.15 and width 29.94 would imply roughly P(Under110.5)=Φ((110.5−110.15)/29.94)≈.505; compared with 0.560 mixed after subjective India-strength/uncertainty scenarios. An unadjusted five-checkpoint mean μX=36.2 with phase SD 13.96 would give P(Over33.5)=Φ((36.2−33.5)/13.96)≈.577, vs .527 adjusted mixture; this small selected powerplay sample is itself incomplete. Thus rank/probabilities **ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT** and not qualified.

**League-only comparator attempt:** ICC T20I 2026 cohort across women's teams and exact 6-over endpoint (with period/venue/strength, pre-cutoff n) NOT_DERIVED. No neutral baseline at these two lines, `BASELINE_P` blank for all four; no fabricated 0.50. No EV as a monetary number because SPORTS_ONLY and no permitted contract price.

| Supplied contract | W conditional full-observation proxy | P (push) | L conditional full-observation proxy | V unconditional | p_card action W/(W+L) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Zimbabwe terminal Under110.5 | .5597 | 0 | .4403 | `UNKNOWN_OPERATOR_ACTION` | .5597 **if complete/action=100%** |
| Zimbabwe terminal Over110.5 | .4403 | 0 | .5597 | `UNKNOWN_OPERATOR_ACTION` | .4403 **if complete/action=100%** |

These W/L masses are conditional on an innings with a usable corresponding endpoint; unconditional match-level W/P/L/V cannot be resolved without interruptions, chase-ending/early-all-out action rates and written contract definitions. Any interpretation of V=0 is a **scoring simplification**, not a verified sportsbook policy. Complement checks .5597+.4403=1; .5267+.4733=1. No push on half-point thresholds; no measured independent cross-quantity joint failure; bounded as above.

#### A6. Inputs, adjustments, adverse mechanisms and winner

| Mechanism | Verified fact | Numeric treatment | Alternative / invalidation |
|---|---|---|---|
| India bowling | First game held Zimbabwe to 114/5, plus recent weaker-opposition T20I conceded figures | centres broadly around 107 Zimbabwe full runs | May be more than single-game variance, not independent 20 powerplay sample |
| Zimbabwe batting | 114 then previous T20Is 142,106,97,151; L20 110.15 | low/base/high spans 90–126 | middle-order rebound or new powerplay high can lift over |
| Powerplay | Last Raipur six-over score 34/1, previous five verified checkpoints avg36.2 | phase base34.5 width13; scenario29/41 | sample selected/incomplete; one boundary flips half-line |
| Player selection | Last-game sides known, Oct18 official XI/toss NOT VERIFIED | uncertainty widened through low/high | later team sheet can materially alter X/Y distribution but cannot be retroactively inserted |
| Venue, dew, weather | Same ground as first game, current strip/at-ball rain not confirmed | no invented numeric effect; broaden scenario | fresh surface or rain changes action and wicket chances |
| Chase mechanics | Batting innings order unverified; if chasing target quickly, innings stops | p values only conditional unconstrained proxy | need target distribution, DLS/overshoot and operator action |
| Winner | India won first match and markedly stronger overall historical T20I results | Qualitative **India Women** | Nyasha Gwanzura 3 wickets triggered 19/4 collapse in previous chase; even a stronger side can lose; win/draw/tie p NOT_ESTIMATED |

**Top failures:** Rank 1 Under110.5 fails if Zimbabwe retain 7+ wickets after 12–14 overs / favorable scoring strip / India rotation; Rank2 Over33.5 fails with a 26–32 powerplay from tight seam bowling and two wickets. No fabricated exact bowling matchup against unconfirmed XI, no unsourced injury declaration and no numeric winner probability.

#### A7. Rule application, selection gates, dependency, alternate hypotheses

- Cricket T20: one innings/side, 20 legal overs maximum, opening powerplay overs 1–6, maximum four overs per bowler under standard ICC T20 rules, innings can terminate all-out, at chase completion or on abandonment/shortening; Super Over to decide a tied match where applicable but not added to first-innings runs. Owner operator terms unverified.
- Ranking uses decreasing unrounded analytical p, not external prices or RM-1 `q` score; no result has been consulted. Low/base/high weights declared before pricing and p_card calculated from common scenario construction. Detailed joint CDF not actually integrated, so only legal Fréchet bound on cross-quantity failures.
- Rank1 .5597 <.62 and margin to Rank2 .0330 <.04; Rank2 .5267 <.58. These must NOT be called qualified high-confidence picks. Other odds/props/handicaps not introduced to fill rank.
- If endpoints strictly require observed complete 20th over (vs any all-out terminal innings) ranking may not be actionable; retain all four as evidence and make explicit action-definition query before settlement. Precision ε≈.003, none of threshold comparisons marginal at this precision.
- `SHADOW_R2_RET04` NONE; `SHADOW_R2_XCARD6` NONE; `SHADOW_RANK_DELTA` NONE; `SHADOW_BASE_ONLY` same order, no PASS. Joint failure of Rank1 & Rank2 bounded [0,.4403], not product. No operator odds; monetary EV N/A under market firewall.
- Unsupported actions do not convert to automatic losses, automatic VOID or fabricated numerical unconditional probabilities.
